# 第 13 章　门扉之启，王座之终

*3.1 版本主视觉　·　图源：米游社*

> Light Slips the Gate, Shadow Greets the Throne　·　门扉之启，王座之终

## 105　Strife, Dispel the Accompanying Fears　纷争啊，伐清湮途的悼惧

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域：Vortex of Genesis　|　角色：Trailblazer; Dan Heng; Aglaea; Trinnon; Castorice; Mydei; Perdikkas; Chartonus; Gnaeus; Phainon　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Hero, Bear Thy Coreflame'' completed

### Story Recap

A mysterious celestial body entangled by Three Paths appears in the Memokeeper's mirror — Amphoreus, The Eternal Land, the next stop for the Trailblaze.

But the landing is not a smooth one. After surviving the crash, the Trailblaze squad meets the local Chrysos Heir heroes. They learn from them that the Titans supporting the world are rotting away, and that the land of Amphoreus is covered in scars.

In Okhema, the final holy city, the two members of the squad undergo great trials and gain the trust of Aglaea, leader of the Chrysos Heirs. Facing a threat from beyond the fog, everyone decides to band together to defeat the Strife Titan Nikador.

In order to destroy Nikador's true form, the group plans to infiltrate Castrum Kremnos of the past to discover the truth. At the end of the Abyss of Fate, the Trailblazer communes with the Time Titan Oronyx, and somehow receives a gaze from Fuli.

With the help of their new friend Mem, everyone pieces together Nikador's divinity and obtains the Titan's Coreflame in the final battle. At last, the young hero Phainon faces his own trial in the holy Vortex of Genesis...

**Divine Echo:** "This is why you slew the Strife Titan, only to reject the Coreflame, letting Strife continue instead..."

- ▸ Is the black tide really that scary?

> ▸ 这黑潮有那么可怕吗？

**Dan Heng:** Since coming to Amphoreus, we have heard many talking about this "black tide." Could you tell us more about it?

**Dan Heng:** Since coming to Amphoreus, we have heard many talking about this "black tide." Could you tell us more about it?

> **丹恒：**来到翁法罗斯后已多次听人提及，能否请各位展开讲讲，这「黑潮」究竟是？

- ▸ I think Gnaeus mentioned it before...

> ▸ 好像听格奈乌斯提起过…

**Castorice:** That is correct. He was willing to entrust the Coreflame to the Chrysos Heirs... because he was also pinning his hopes on us, to succeed him in shouldering the responsibility of resisting the black tide.

> **遐蝶：**没错，他愿意将火种交给黄金裔…也是寄希望于我们能继续肩负起抗击黑潮的重任。

**Dan Heng:** Could you tell us more about this "black tide"?

> **丹恒：**能否请各位展开讲讲，这「黑潮」究竟是？

**Aglaea:** It is one of the culprits that drives the people of Amphoreus to madness. It was during the Era Bellica that this... unspeakable thing first appeared, like a plague, alongside the Three Titans of Calamity.

> **阿格莱雅：**是让翁法罗斯众生陷入疯狂的元凶之一。自纷争世起，此种不可名状之物便如同瘟疫，随灾厄三泰坦一同降临大地……

**Aglaea:** Nobody knows where it came from or how it came to be. What we do know, however, is that it is steadily encroaching upon what little remains of this tranquil land, much like a rising sea closing in on an island from all sides. It is for this very reason that it became known as the "black tide."

> **阿格莱雅：**我们不知它的源头从何而来，反而发觉它如同包围陆地的大海一般，持续进逼着尚且安宁却已然狭小的土地。也正因此，它才得名为「黑潮」。

**Aglaea:** Those who succumb to it lose their reason and humanity, becoming vicious, mindless monsters. Mortals are not the only ones susceptible to its influence — even gods and Titans cannot escape from it.

> **阿格莱雅：**凡受其影响者无不变得扭曲狰狞，理性丧失，人性泯灭，化为无血无泪的野兽。而这并非凡人独有的厄运，就连泰坦诸神也无法幸免。

**Castorice:** According to Gnaeus, Nikador, though one of the embodiments of Calamity, also bears the responsibility of fending off the black tide...

> **遐蝶：**根据格奈乌斯的说法，尼卡多利虽是灾厄的化身之一，却也肩负着抵御黑潮的使命……

**Castorice:** Perhaps that is also why Lord Phainon threw himself into the trial of Strife so readily.

> **遐蝶：**或许，这也是另一个原因…让白厄阁下会义无反顾地投入「纷争」的试炼。

**Aglaea:** But now, we must look for alternative means of igniting the flames of Strife.

> **阿格莱雅：**而现在，我们必须另寻他法，尝试点燃「纷争」了。

**Mydei:** So, Aglaea, what should we do now?

> **万敌：**说吧，阿格莱雅，下一步怎么走？

**Aglaea:** You know very well the decision doesn't lie with me.

> **阿格莱雅：**你知道，这不取决于我。

**Aglaea:** As the successor to Kremnos, Mydei, you know better than anyone that if it were simply about emerging triumphant on the battlefield of Strife, you and your people could manage it with ease.

> **阿格莱雅：**万敌，你身为悬锋的继业者，自然明白：若只是在「纷争」的疆场上取胜，对你们而言易如反掌……

**Aglaea:** But ultimately... the heavy burden of divinity can only be borne by one.

> **阿格莱雅：**但神明职责的重量，终究只能由一人背负。

**Aglaea:** It is foolhardiness and indecision, not the unclaimed seat of divinity, that pushes this world into the deathly fog.

> **阿格莱雅：**将世界推入死雾的是鲁莽和犹疑，而非悬置的神性。

**Mydei:** ...

> **万敌：**……

### Return to your private bath chamber and discuss the present circumstances with Dan Heng

> *After ensuring Phainon's safety, (Trailblazer) and I retreated to the bath chamber to rest. We also drew our own conclusions about the present situation in the world of Amphoreus*

**Dan Heng:** The black tide...

> **丹恒：**黑潮……

*(Investigate the door, optional)*

Phainon was defeated in the Trial of Strife and had his consciousness corroded by the Titan. It will be some time until he recovers... Let the frustrated hero rest a while.

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Dan Heng:** An "unspeakable" thing of "unknown origin"... What comes to your mind when we put these keywords together?

> **丹恒：**「不可名状」、「不知源头」…这些关键词组合在一起，你会想到什么？

- ▸ A Stellaron?

> ▸ 星核？

**Dan Heng:** That's right. If you think about the various characteristics of Amphoreus — an isolated world of eternal night being eroded by an unknown power... we cannot discount this possibility.

> **丹恒：**不错，这是一种可能性。再回想下翁法罗斯的特征——一个「陷入永夜」、「与世隔绝」且「遭到不知名力量蚕食」的世界……

- ▸ An Aeon?

> ▸ 星神？

**Dan Heng:** That's right. If you think about the various characteristics of Amphoreus — an isolated world of eternal night being eroded by an unknown power... we cannot discount this possibility.

> **丹恒：**不错，这是一种可能性。再回想下翁法罗斯的特征——一个「陷入永夜」、「与世隔绝」且「遭到不知名力量蚕食」的世界……

- ▸ Uhh... a Leviathan? A cosmic mega octopus?

> ▸ 呃…古兽？银河超巨大章鱼？

**Dan Heng:** ...A hypothesis like that has never occurred to me, I must admit. But considering how the Leviathan hasn't been sighted in the universe for hundreds of Amber Eras, I think the possibility of it being the culprit is minuscule.

> **丹恒：**这倒是个未曾设想的假说…不过依我见，古兽已在寰宇间销声匿迹近百余纪元，他们是罪魁祸首的概率微乎其微。

**Dan Heng:** Let's think about the various characteristics of Amphoreus instead. An isolated world of eternal night being eroded by an unknown power...

> **丹恒：**再回想下翁法罗斯的特征吧——这是一个「陷入永夜」、「与世隔绝」且「遭到不知名力量蚕食」的世界……

**Dan Heng:** This has been bothering me for a while now. Everything we have seen thus far suggests that Amphoreus wasn't always isolated from the rest of the universe. Be it the birth of the Titans or the encroaching black tide, none of these can be explained as a "natural progression" of a civilization.

> **丹恒：**我先前就很在意，种种迹象表明，翁法罗斯绝不是一个从未接触过宇宙的世界。无论是泰坦的降生，还是黑潮来袭…都难以用文明的自然发展解释。

**Dan Heng:** There must be other reasons for its current state of isolation. It's just... if the Cancer of All Worlds is truly the culprit, then we should have observed certain phenomena — explicit or otherwise — by now, but that isn't the case.

> **丹恒：**它如今的「封闭」一定另有隐情。只是，如果原因也是万界之癌…有些直接或间接的迹象还未得到印证。

- ▸ The Voice of the Stellaron?

> ▸ 星核之声？

**Dan Heng:** That's what I was thinking too. From past experience, the Stellaron within you should be able to resonate with other Stellarons, but we haven't encountered anything like that here.

> **丹恒：**想到一起去了。依经验来看，你体内的星核应该能与同类产生共鸣。但目前还未出现这种征兆。

- ▸ The Antimatter Legion?

> ▸ 反物质军团？

**Dan Heng:** That's what I was thinking too. From past experience, the Destruction has always been involved in some way when it comes to a world corrupted by a Stellaron.

> **丹恒：**想到一起去了。依经验来看，遭到星核污染的世界，多少都与「毁灭」有点关系。

- ▸ The Stellaron Hunters?

> ▸ 星核猎手？

**Dan Heng:** That's what I was thinking too. From past experience, Stellaron Hunters are certain to appear on a world with a Stellaron.

> **丹恒：**想到一起去了。依经验来看，凡有星核的世界，必定能见到星核猎手出没。

**Dan Heng:** There are several other unanswered questions, too. My gut feeling is that things here are not as simple as they seem...

> **丹恒：**不仅如此，还有其他疑点悬而未决。直觉告诉我，恐怕没那么简单……

**Dan Heng:** Furthermore, what happened in Penacony also proved that the appearance of a world may not necessarily be due to the influence of just one Path.

> **丹恒：**匹诺康尼的历史也证明了，一个世界的样貌未必是一条命途作用的结果。

- ▸ No sweat! The Trailblaze can tackle even ten Paths if it must!

> ▸ 无所谓，「开拓」会打十个。

**Dan Heng:** *sigh* It's good to be motivated at least.

> **丹恒：**哎…有气势是好事。

- ▸ It could be an all-star cast.

> ▸ 这大抵也是一种全明星吧。

**Dan Heng:** Heh... In another sense, it's Penacony all over again.

> **丹恒：**呵…另一种意义上的匹诺康尼啊。

- ▸ Who knows, maybe Akivili's behind it all.

> ▸ 没准是阿基维利自产自销呢。

> **丹恒：**我想…就连虚构史学家也不敢轻易采用这种设定。

**Dan Heng:** For now, let's just take things as they come. The black tide could be a good starting point. Since Nikador has fallen, those who were suppressed by them will no doubt begin to counterattack now.

> **丹恒：**事已至此，边走边看吧。就把黑潮当作一个突破口，尼卡多利既已陨落，受其压制的力量也必将开始反扑……

**Dan Heng:** It seems our trailblazing expedition is growing in significance...

> **丹恒：**看来…我们的「开拓」之旅，意义愈发重大了啊。

> **万敌：**我想与你讨论…悬锋族人的未来。

**Krateros:** Interesting. I heard the Coreflame of the Lance of Fury has been restored — though not by your hand — and you're even considering giving up Nikador's divine power. Is this true?

> **克拉特鲁斯：**有趣。我听闻「天谴之矛」的火种已然归位，却并非由你亲手交还。你甚至想将尼卡多利的神力拱手相让——此事当真？

**Mydei:** ...

> **万敌：**……

**Mydei:** Those rumors are not entirely groundless.

> **万敌：**那些传闻，并非臆造。

**Krateros:** In other words... you did entertain the thought of giving up the throne of Kremnos and forsaking your people.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**言外之意…你曾想过放弃悬锋的王权，放弃领导你的族人。

**Mydei:** The divine power is not equivalent to the throne. The path of Strife is also not our people's only way—

> **万敌：**神力不等同于王权，我族的出路也不止「纷争」一条——

**Krateros:** ...Enough with the word games! You, of all people, should understand the plight of the Kremnoans!

> **克拉特鲁斯：**——文字游戏！你对悬锋人的境遇心知肚明。

**Krateros:** Think about all the warriors of the Kremnoan detachment who left their homeland with you! Think about their children! ...Only the yearning for our former glory days has kept us going all this time.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**随你一同离开故乡的悬锋孤军，还有他们的后人…支撑我们走到现在的，正是对旧日荣光的渴望。

**Krateros:** If they ever learn that the crown prince they look up to so much is actually toying with the notion of stepping down... think of the disgrace it would bring upon your departed mother, Mydeimos.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**假如让他们知道，备受族人尊崇的王储萌生了放弃的念头…迈德漠斯，你该如何向你逝去的母亲交代？

**Krateros:** You'd best remember that she died for you because of Eurypon's schemes! To restore the glory of Kremnos!

> **克拉特鲁斯：**别忘了，她可是为了你——为了重振悬锋的荣光——才会死于欧利庞的毒计！

**Mydei:** Since you've brought that up... Tell me, who is responsible for my father's death?

> **万敌：**既然话已说到这个份上…我问你，是谁杀死了父亲？

**Krateros:** Are you trying to shame me, Mydeimos? I may be old, but I haven't become so decrepit that I've forgotten it all. Of course I remember that Eurypon died when the Kremnoan detachment surrounded him.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**你是在羞辱我吗，迈德漠斯？我是老了，但还没老到愚痴。我当然记得，欧利庞死于悬锋孤军的围剿……

**Krateros:** You drove the spear into his chest with your very own hands.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**是你亲手用长矛贯穿了他的胸膛。

**Mydei:** That was merely the final outcome.

> **万敌：**但那只是结果。

**Mydei:** Do you still not see it? What led my parents and our people to their downfall is none other than this Strife we speak of. The quest for glory, the tenet of "Valorous Death Before Glorious Return"...

> **万敌：**你还没看清吗，吾师？将我的父母，还有悬锋一族送上末路的，正是你我口中的「纷争」，对所谓荣光的追逐，一声声「宁战死，毋荣归」的口号……

**Mydei:** This is the creed of every Kremnoan, no matter whether these beliefs are justified or not.

> **万敌：**是每一个悬锋人的执念，无论它正确与否。

**Krateros:** ...

> **克拉特鲁斯：**……

**Krateros:** Don't be ridiculous. The only thing that could slay a king is a spear... Even an infant knows that much.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**可笑。能杀死一位王的只有长枪，就连悬锋的婴儿都明白这个道理。

**Krateros:** I swore to your mother that I would guard your crown with my life... I never foresaw that the son of Gorgo himself would be the first to turn his back on the Kremnoan spirit.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**我曾向你的母亲发誓，要以生命捍卫你的王冠。但我未曾想过，歌耳戈之子会带头质疑…背叛悬锋精神。

**Krateros:** Gorgo... Your mother bore that name, the name of the great founder of Castrum Kremnos, and she upheld that name with honor. If you choose to turn your back on her legacy like a wretched deserter... do not fault me for withholding my loyalty, Mydeimos.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**「歌耳戈」…你的母亲与悬锋城的建立者同名，她的作为也配得上这名字的重量。但你——你若要远离她的荣光，做个逃兵…恕我无法再献上自己的忠诚，迈德漠斯。

**Mydei:** I came to discuss the future of our people with you, but... it appears this is not the right time yet.

> **万敌：**我本想与你共议族人的未来。但…现在似乎不是时候。

**Krateros:** Go, Mydei... Hah, that's what those Chrysos Heirs call you, isn't it? Go down the path you have deemed correct. Deep down, you have always been one to do as you please, and I am well aware that no one can deter you once you've made up your mind.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**走吧，万敌——那些黄金裔是这么称呼你的——走你自以为正确的道路。你骨子里是个我行我素的战士，一旦下定决心，没人能阻止你。

**Krateros:** But don't you ever dream of renouncing the traditions of Kremnos... We may reside in Okhema now, but we remain eternally bound to Strife by blood.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**可是不要妄图否定悬锋的传统…我们身在奥赫玛，但永远是「纷争」的子民。

**Mydei:** Bloodlines no longer hold any significance in this era of destruction.

> **万敌：**在行将毁灭的时代，谈论血统没有意义。

**Krateros:** My young lord, always bear this in mind: Never reveal your vulnerabilities to anyone. A lion should never dwell amidst its prey...

> **克拉特鲁斯：**少主，记住：切勿将软肋示人，一头雄狮绝不应混迹于它的猎物之中……

**Krateros:** ...Especially when it wields power enough to dominate the entire hunting ground.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**尤其，是它足以主宰整片猎场的时候。

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: Eurypon, the boy did nothing wrong!

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: This is murder! Do not sully the noble name of Kremnos with the blood of an innocent child! You mustn't reveal your weaknesses to the enemy!

**Old King Eurypon:** Enough! My decision is final. We must use this child's life to preserve the soul of the great Nikador.

> **先王欧利庞：**够了！朕心意已决。尼卡多利的战魂必须用这孩子的命延续。

**Old King Eurypon:** We must... save Castrum Kremnos.

> **先王欧利庞：**我们…必须拯救悬锋城。

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: Don't be absurd! Those tiny hands lack the strength to even lift a spear! Do you truly believe him a threat to Castrum Kremnos!?

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: You'll be ending the bloodline of Kremnos if you do this. Our millennia-old glory will become nothing more than a joke!

**Old King Eurypon:** Have you forgotten that we Kremnoans place no value in bloodlines? It is through pure bloodshed and slaughter that the kings of Kremnos have ascended to the throne...

> **先王欧利庞：**你忘了，悬锋人不相信血脉。我们的王只能从浴血的角斗中诞生……

**Old King Eurypon:** As for our millennia-old "glory"... it has always been a joke in my eyes. Glorify it all you want, but it will not alter the nature of slaughter. Those who revel in it are but murderers. They are no more noble than the most monstrous beasts in Amphoreus.

> **先王欧利庞：**至于悬锋城千百年的「荣光」，那本来就是个笑话——杀戮就是杀戮，为虚荣而高兴的杀人凶手，并不比翁法罗斯最凶蛮的野兽更高贵。

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: Enough with your words! Among all the kings of Kremnos, which one did not inherit the crown only after plucking it from the lifeless body of his father?

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: But listen to yourself now! Do you truly believe you can cleanse the blood from your hands through mere words alone?

**Old King Eurypon:** Nay, quite the contrary. I will end this cycle of bloodshed...

> **先王欧利庞：**不，恰恰相反：我正要结束这血腥的循环……

**Old King Eurypon:** ...Starting with this child. With these bloodstained hands, I will end it all.

> **先王欧利庞：**就从这孩子开始——就在我染血的双手中结束。

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: Your failure is inevitable, Eurypon! You are just a coward who can only raise his blade against his own child! A beast that murders his own kin!

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: Royal Wing Elites and Ephors of Kremnos, you cannot sit idly by and watch this atrocity unfold! If you consider yourselves the honorable sons and daughters of Gorgo, then take up your spears and join me in putting an end to this bloody charade!

**Old King Eurypon:** ...Does anyone else have any objections? If so, come forward with your spear and prove your mettle!

> **先王欧利庞：**可还有人…提出异议？若有，就拿上铜枪，上前来证明自己！

**Magistrate:** ...Understood.

> **裁判官：**…是。

**Magistrate:** Five...

> **裁判官：**五……

**Crowds:** ...

> **众人：**……

**Magistrate:** Four...

> **裁判官：**四……

**Krateros:** ...

> **克拉特鲁斯：**……

**Magistrate:** Three...

> **裁判官：**三……

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: No one dares stand by my side? Cowards! Has that crown stripped you of your courage!?

**Magistrate:** Two...

> **裁判官：**二……

**Crowds:** ...

> **众人：**……

**Magistrate:** One.

> **裁判官：**一。

**Old King Eurypon:** It appears there are no objections. Then, in accordance with the Kremnoan Council's principle of tacit consent, the decision is final.

> **先王欧利庞：**看来无人提出异议。基于悬锋元老院「一致沉默」之原则，此事已无复议必要。

**Old King Eurypon:** With all present as witnesses, this child shall descend into the Sea of Souls, where he will nourish the Lance of Fury and join the fallen heroes.

> **先王欧利庞：**在列位公民见证下，此子将沉入冥海：以飨「天谴之矛」，并与天地间众英灵。

**Old King Eurypon:** May fate and the gods await your arrival...

> **先王欧利庞：**愿命运与尊神恭候你……

**Old King Eurypon:** My son... Mydeimos.

> **先王欧利庞：**吾儿…迈德漠斯。

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: Stop!

**Old King Eurypon:** If you have more to say, then speak. On account of the life we have shared together, I grant you this one final chance.

> **先王欧利庞：**说吧。看在你我同出生、共入死的份上…朕给你最后一次机会。

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: With the Blade of Fury above us and the Council bearing witness, I, Gorgo, hereby move to challenge the crown in accordance with the laws of Kremnos!

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: O unworthy king, in the name of the Council of Elders, I demand that you duel me!

**Old King Eurypon:** Heh... Very well.

> **先王欧利庞：**呵…也好。

**Old King Eurypon:** We shall see who the Kremnoan blade strikes down.

> **先王欧利庞：**那就来看看…悬锋将落向谁的头颅吧。

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: Mydeimos, my son...

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: They all tell me to forget... But how could I ever?

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: The son of Gorgo... will be crowned in blood.

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: If there is no Kremnos without the crown... then I shall seize the crown and smash it to pieces to bring the people to their senses.

The mother roared in anger.

The armies remained in silence.

Only the cries and the waves of the Sea of Souls echoed.

Returning to the Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

## 107　Grove, Wherefore Are the Wise Silent　谧林啊，寤思者缘何失语

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域："Eternal Holy City" Okhema　|　角色：Trailblazer; Castorice; Trianne; Tribbie; Merchant; Aglaea; Dan Heng; Hyacine; Clementine; Mem; Anaxa; Cerces; Mnestia; Flame Reaver　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Glory, Turn From Imbibed Poison'' completed

### Speak with Castorice

> *Aglaea shows sincere gratitude for our help in defeating Nikador, preparing generous gifts and inviting (Trailblazer) and I to the Hero's Bath for a discussion.*

Now, in your private bath chamber

Ever since the battle with Nikador, you haven't slept so soundly in ages.

Even if Death itself came to rouse you, you'd proudly retort: Why does life slumber? Because the bed is warm...

Yet, a chill from the River of Souls wafted by, a sensation hauntingly familiar. Could Death truly be at your door?

Best to open your eyes, just in case...

**Castorice:** Mr./Miss (Trailblazer), you're awake.

> **遐蝶：**开拓者阁下，你醒了。

- ▸ ...No wonder I felt a chill.

**Castorice:** Is the room too cold?

> **遐蝶：**是说房间太冷了么？

- ▸ ...Castorice, why does life slumber?

**Castorice:** ...That's a complicated question. I'm not sure I can answer that.

> **遐蝶：**…是个很深奥的问题，我恐怕难以解答。

- ▸ ...Why are you here?

**Castorice:** I apologize for arriving uninvited. I saw that you were still asleep, so I took the liberty of waiting nearby.

> **遐蝶：**恕我不请自来…见你还未起床，只好在一旁静静等待。

**Castorice:** I bring a message from Lady Aglaea. She has prepared a gift for you and Dan Heng, and wishes to present it to you in person.

> **遐蝶：**阿格莱雅大人遣我来向阁下转达：她为二位开拓者备了礼物，希望能当面交付。

- ▸ A gift? Gimme, gimme!
- ▸ What are we waiting for? Let's go!

**Castorice:** I'm glad you're in good spirits. Alright, let's set off.

> **遐蝶：**看起来很有精神呢。那我们走吧。

### Follow Castorice to meet with Aglaea

*(While walking with Castorice)*

**Castorice:** The baths are bustling. Now that Nikador has fallen, the soldiers in the city can finally catch a break.

> **遐蝶：**浴场都是人呢。尼卡多利被击败后，城中战士终于可以放松些时日了。

**Castorice:** Is that... Lady Tribbie and Lady Trianne?

> **遐蝶：**那是…缇宝大人和缇安大人？

*(Approach Tribbie and Trianne)*

**Trianne:** You must have forgotten! This is a precious relic from our hometown! We definitely all saw it back then!

> **缇安：**绝对，是你忘记了！这就是*我们*，见过的！家乡的宝贝！

**Tribbie:** Trianne, this is a counterfeit. We both know it's impossible for something this fragile to have survived all those years in one piece...

> **缇宝：**缇安，这不是真品。*我们*都知道的，这种易碎的制品，不可能保存完好至今……

**Trianne:** Nothing is impossible! We are fragile, too! But we still got through those... those sad times. Look at that color... and how it glitters in the light... It's exactly as we remember it!

> **缇安：**什么都有可能！*我们*也很易碎，但都从那段…悲伤的历史中，坚持下来了！你看它的颜色，在光芒下！亮晶晶的…和*我们*记忆里，一模一样！

**Trianne:** Why won't you buy it? If someone else gets to it before us, we'll... we'll miss our chance forever...

> **缇安：**为什么，你不愿意买下它呢？要是明天它被别人买走了，*我们*、*我们*可能就再也没机会……

**Tribbie:** Calm down, Trianne... You need rest.

> **缇宝：**缇安…别激动，你现在需要好好休息……

**Merchant:** Please, there's no need to fret. If you need more time to make a decision, I can put this aside for you until tomorrow... or until you're ready.

> **商人：**两位，息怒。如果大人们还想考虑考虑，我可以把它留到明天…不，留到两位考虑好，如何？

**Tribbie:** No, no, it's alright. Trianne is just... She'll come to her senses tomorrow. We'll be off first.

> **缇宝：**不、不必啦。缇安她只是…她明天就会想明白了，先不打扰你做生意。

**Trianne:** Trianne is just what? Trianne is super mad, that's what!

> **缇安：**缇安只是什么？缇安只是很生气！

**Trianne:** You've forgotten... You've forgotten all about Mama! Mama gave us something just like this before!

> **缇安：**因为你忘了，你忘记了妈妈！妈妈给*我们*，送过一样的东西！

**Tribbie:** Trianne...

> **缇宝：**缇安……

(Trianne runs off)

**Merchant:** Uh... See you tomorrow? I hope you two patch things up soon...

> **商人：**那…明天见？祝两位明天和好如初……

**Tribbie:** See you tomorrow. May the winds of fortune bless you.

> **缇宝：**嗯，明天见。也祝你生意兴隆。

### Find out about what's happening from the merchants

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Merchant:** It's so hard to understand what they're thinking sometimes…

> **商人：**唉，两位大人…真让人摸不清头脑。

*(Speak to the merchant)*

**Castorice:** Excuse me. If you don't mind, could you tell me what Lady Tribbie and Lady Trianne were discussing just now?

> **遐蝶：**请问，刚才缇安大人和缇宝大人在谈些什么？

**Merchant:** Oh, it's Miss Castorice! It's been a while! Well, honestly, I'm not quite sure myself.

> **商人：**遐蝶小姐，好久不见。唉，说实话，我也有些搞不清楚状况。

**Merchant:** See, I have a little hobby of collecting handicrafts. Some time ago, I found this trinket being sold at the Marmoreal Market and bought it to use as decoration at my stall.

> **商人：**我平素喜欢收集工艺品，前些日子在云石市集上看见有人兜售这个小东西，就出价买了下来，摆在小铺当作装饰品。

**Merchant:** When Lady Tribbie and Lady Trianne walked by earlier, Lady Trianne happened to catch sight of it and promptly insisted that it was a precious memento from her hometown.

> **商人：**刚才二位大人路过，缇安大人一眼就瞧见了它，嚷着说是什么家乡的宝贝。

**Merchant:** But Lady Tribbie believed it to be a counterfeit. She said it couldn't possibly be from Janusopolis — the likelihood of such fragile glassware surviving the Chrysos War is simply too low.

> **商人：**但缇宝大人觉得只是仿制品，说要真是雅努萨波利斯的手艺，玻璃制品如此易碎，怎么可能从黄金战争的动荡里幸存下来？

**Merchant:** For some reason, Lady Trianne became awfully upset after hearing that.

> **商人：**不知为何，缇安大人似乎很生气。

**Castorice:** I see...

> **遐蝶：**这样啊……

**Castorice:** Madam, would you mind selling this trinket to me?

> **遐蝶：**阁下，我有意买下此物…能否请你割爱？

**Merchant:** Certainly. It's just... if it truly is a relic from Janusopolis, it could be worth a small fortune... Miss Castorice, I'm running a business here, after all, so...

> **商人：**这倒无妨。但这若真是雅努萨波利斯的遗物，恐怕价值不菲。遐蝶小姐，我毕竟是个商人，您看……

**Castorice:** I understand. Feel free to name your price.

> **遐蝶：**我理解，尽管出价吧。

- ▸ Not so fast! I object to this!

**Merchant:** And what might your concerns be, my friend?

> **商人：**这位朋友，你又有何高见？

- ▸ You've got some nerve trying to rip off a Chrysos Heir.
- ▸ Tribbie already called it a fake! Are you trying to pull a fast one on us?

**Merchant:** I... Fine. I'll sell it to you at cost as a sign of goodwill, Miss Castorice. Just promise to support my business in the future!

> **商人：**这…也罢，那我就依成本价，送遐蝶小姐一个顺水人情吧。希望以后，多多支持我的小店哈。

- ▸ (Glare at her in silence.)

**Merchant:** Uh... I suppose I can sell it to you at cost as a sign of goodwill, Miss Castorice. Just promise to support my business in the future!

> **商人：**这…我要不就依成本价，送遐蝶小姐一个顺水人情吧。希望以后，多多支持我的小店哈。

**Castorice:** Much appreciated.

*(If you vocally objected)*

**Castorice:** Thanks for helping me out. I'm not one to venture out often, and while I have some savings, it takes a lot out of me to even talk to people, let alone haggle... Had you not been here, I would've been set back plenty.

*(If you glared in silence)*

**Castorice:** We're lucky that she has a good heart. I'm not one to venture out often, and while I have some savings, it takes a lot out of me to even talk to people, let alone haggle... Had it been another merchant, I would've been set back plenty.

> **遐蝶：**幸好遇见了好心人。我平日深居简出，虽说有点积蓄，但…确实不擅长与他人打交道，更遑论购物还价了。换作其他商贩，恐怕是要破费了。

**Castorice:** The authenticity of the piece isn't important. What matters is that it holds special meaning for Lady Trianne and reminds her of her hometown.

> **遐蝶：**这件工艺品的真伪并不重要。重要的是它对缇安大人有特别的意义，能让她回忆起故乡。

**Castorice:** I've long wished to show my gratitude for Lady Trianne's care, but I haven't been able to come up with any ideas for a suitable gift. This chance encounter today feels like divine intervention. Perhaps this is the gift I've been searching for.

> **遐蝶：**我平日受缇安大人诸多照顾，一直想找机会给缇安大人备点薄礼以示谢意，只是不知要送些什么好。今天碰巧撞见此事，也算命运暗中相助，就选它做礼物吧。

**Castorice:** Still, this trinket is admittedly rather fragile. I will have to make some adjustments and stitch it onto wool felt to protect it. That way, no matter how much Lady Trianne runs around, it won't break easily.

> **遐蝶：**…只是，这小玩意确实十分易碎。我打算回去后稍作加工，把它缝装到羊毛毡里，这样缇安大人再活泼也不用担心打碎它了。

### Continue following Castorice to meet with Aglaea

*(While walking with Castorice)*

**Castorice:** Alright, back to business after that little detour.

> **遐蝶：**好啦，这算是路上的小插曲。

**Castorice:** Let's keep moving. Don't stray far now — we've already kept Lady Aglaea waiting long enough.

> **遐蝶：**我们接着赶路吧，阁下请跟紧我——阿格莱雅女士已经等了些时候了。

### Speak with Aglaea

**Castorice:** It seems your friend arrived before us.

> **遐蝶：**看来，阁下的朋友早已到了。

*(Speak with Aglaea)*

### Step onto the Woven Trail and enter the Grove of Epiphany

### Help March 7th take a picture of the giant tree as a keepsake

*(Dialogue continues from the previous step)*

A giant tree bowed slightly in the silent forest, tilting and falling into the wilting green waves. A passing breeze rustled through the leaves, as if it was sighing, softly fading into the lightless ocean above on high.

Would March 7th appreciate this sublime scene? You're uncertain. Dan Heng, perhaps...

Never mind, every time he encounters a tree as big as this one, it would be a deeply troubling situation.

> **遐蝶：**开拓者阁下，我们一起上吧。

*(Begin battle against Tide-Eroded Blade ×3)*

**Castorice:** As expected... the words "black," "sword," and "cloak"...

> **遐蝶：**果然，「黑色」的「利剑」和「斗篷」……

**Castorice:** ...They're talking about the black tide.

> **遐蝶：**…指的就是「黑潮」。

*(After the battle)*

**Trianne:** Y—You defeated it! Little Gray, Little Cas, you're so strong...

> **缇安：**干、干掉了！小小灰，小小蝶，好厉害……

**Mem:** But these monsters... They aren't supposed to show up here, right?

> **迷迷：**可是，这些黑乎乎的家伙…不该出现在这里吧？

**Castorice:** To think the black tide has spread this quickly to the Grove despite its remote location...

> **遐蝶：**虽然树庭所处地理位置相对偏僻，但黑潮竟能迅速蔓延至此……

**Castorice:** I'm afraid a world where Strife has fallen is far more dangerous than we ever imagined.

> **遐蝶：**「纷争」失守的世界，恐怕要比我们预想得更凶险。

**Trianne:** Oh no... Does this mean that Okhema is also in danger?

> **缇安：**呜，那奥赫玛岂不是也危险了？

**Castorice:** Looking at this mess... it seems the scholars at least succeeded in fending off the first wave of attacks.

> **遐蝶：**单看这一地狼藉…学者们应该至少挡住了第一波进攻。

**Castorice:** But... at what price?

> **遐蝶：**只是，不知道付出了多少代价……

*(Dialogue continues into the next step)*

### Report the situation to Aglaea

*(Dialogue continues from the previous step)*

At the same time, in Okhema

**Hyacine:** Lady Aglaea, after a few days of surgeries both major and minor, we were able to save the life of that patient found on the outskirts of Okhema.

> **风堇：**阿格莱雅女士，那名出现在奥赫玛城郊的伤患，经过几日大小手术，已经性命无虞了。

**Aglaea:** Thank you again, Hyacine, for your hard work.

> **阿格莱雅：**平白又为你添了辛劳，风堇。

**Hyacine:** The scarring of his organs was especially severe, a result of the black tide's corruption... It's a miracle he's even survived. I can't say for sure when he'll regain consciousness...

> **风堇：**他受黑潮影响，脏器的纤维化尤为严重，能活下来已经是奇迹，我不能保证他何时能够醒来……

**Aglaea:** Don't stress yourself out over this. You've already done everything in your power to save him. There's no need to hurry to the Grove either. You deserve a good rest now.

> **阿格莱雅：**别有压力，你已尽了你的职责。现在也别着急回树庭了，先好好休息吧。

**Aglaea:** Tribbie, I have some things to tell you as well... There's something strange about where that patient came from.

> **阿格莱雅：**吾师，我还有些事想与你说。那名伤者的来历…有些蹊跷。

**Aglaea:** He is not Okheman. The way the stitches on his clothes are hidden is quite unique... Such craftsmanship is undeniably from the Grove.

> **阿格莱雅：**他并非奥赫玛人。其身上所着衣裳，针脚的藏埋方式颇为特殊…是树庭的手艺。

**Tribbie:** The Grove? You mean...

> **缇宝：**树庭？难道说……

**Aglaea:** Now that I think about it, Castorice and the rest should have arrived at the Grove by now.

> **阿格莱雅：**算算时间，遐蝶她们也该到了。

**Tribbie:** Ah! Trianne just got in contact!

> **缇宝：**啊…缇安来消息了！

**Tribbie:** Mmm...

> **缇宝：**唔……

**Tribbie:** The black tide has invaded the Grove...

> **缇宝：** *我们*…*我们*看到树庭已遭黑潮造物入侵……

**Tribbie:** We haven't detected any survivors, even with the help of the golden thread... Wh—What should we do, Agy?

> **缇宝：**透过金线，也暂时还没有感知到幸存者的存在…怎、怎么办，阿雅？

**Aglaea:** ...The black tide is posing an even greater risk to us now. I did not expect the Grove to be the first to be impacted, though.

> **阿格莱雅：**…黑潮威胁果真加剧了，只没想到竟是树庭首当其冲。

**Aglaea:** Can they tell where the Coreflame is right now?

> **阿格莱雅：**他们/她们能感知到火种如今身在何方么？

**Tribbie:** Mmm...

> **缇宝：**呜……

**Tribbie:** We see it... The Coreflame is still there, but...

> **缇宝：** *我们*看到了…火种还在，只是……

**Aglaea:** But what?

> **阿格莱雅：**只是？

**Tribbie:** But... they can't say for sure whether the Titan has also been corrupted by the black tide.

> **缇宝：**只是…他们/她们无法确认泰坦有没有也受到黑潮感染……

**Aglaea:** Ah...

> **阿格莱雅：**哎……

**Aglaea:** Please let them know that for now, the most pressing task is to identify the Coreflame and transfer it to a safe place as quickly as possible. We must also search for survivors and find out exactly what happened to the Grove.

> **阿格莱雅：**当务之急是尽快确认并转移火种，搜寻幸存者，以便厘清树庭之灾始末…就这么转告他们/她们吧。

**Aglaea:** Oh, and... find Anaxa. Unless something went terribly wrong, he should still be alive.

> **阿格莱雅：**还有——务必确认那刻夏的下落。如无意外，他一定还活着。

**Tribbie:** They've set off. Please, stay safe, everyone...

> **缇宝：**她们出发了。但愿大家平安无事……

**Aglaea:** I pray for their safety, too.

> **阿格莱雅：**…但愿吧。

**Tribbie:** Agy... how do you know that Anaxa is still alive?

> **缇宝：**不过，阿雅…你怎么知道那刻夏还活着？

**Aglaea:** I know that man all too well. If that blasphemous "performer" knew that he was not long for this world, he would most certainly try to seize the Coreflame and drag the Titan down with him.

> **阿格莱雅：**我对他的秉性再清楚不过。倘若这位渎神的「大表演家」知道自己命不久矣，定会打起火种的主意，教泰坦为自己陪葬。

**Tribbie:** Is this... a joke as well?

> **缇宝：**这也是个…玩笑吗？

**Aglaea:** No, I'm being serious this time.

> **阿格莱雅：**不，这次是认真的。

**Aglaea:** We're facing threats from both within and without Okhema. The merciless carnage may have ceased, but Okhema's nemeses continue to sharpen their blades even now...

> **阿格莱雅：**内忧外患呵。痛苦的残杀虽已辍停，奥赫玛的宿敌却在我们眼下砥砺锋芒至今……

**Aglaea:** They may have even prepared the gallows from which to hang us already.

> **阿格莱雅：**现在，他们或已为我们备好绞索了。

**Tribbie:** You mean...

> **缇宝：**你说的是……

**Aglaea:** Even if the divine authority of Strife is as yet unclaimed, the black tide is advancing more rapidly than expected... I fear this tragedy is more than a mere natural disaster.

> **阿格莱雅：**即便「纷争」之神权悬置，这道黑色巨浪未免也来得太过迅猛…我猜这场惨剧不只是天灾。

**Aglaea:** Alas, it's too late. The Council will likely take this opportunity to do something... I will try to keep this under wraps for now, but you must also take care and be prepared for anything that may happen.

> **阿格莱雅：**可惜，既已事发，只怕元老院又会乘机有所动作…我会尽可能封锁消息，你也要留心，防患于未然。

**Tribbie:** Alright. Should I... inform Hyacine about this?

> **缇宝：**嗯。那…还要告诉小风堇么？

**Aglaea:** Not for now... She must be exhausted after performing surgeries non-stop for so many days. I don't want to overwhelm her with this bad news.

> **阿格莱雅：**晚些吧…她连日进行手术，已经疲惫不堪。我不想她被这突如其来的噩耗压垮。

**Tribbie:** ...We can only hope that the Grove weathers this crisis with as many survivors as possible.

> **缇宝：**唉…希望树庭的人们，度过这次难关的…越多越好。

**Aglaea:** That being said, based on the condition of this patient, Tribbie, we should prepare for the worst.

> **阿格莱雅：**吾师，只看这名伤患的状况，你我应做好最坏的打算……

**Aglaea:** ...It's likely he is the only one who managed to escape from the Grove, against all odds.

> **阿格莱雅：**…除他拼死逃出，树庭恐怕凶多吉少。

### Keep going and investigate the cause of the calamity

> *Black tide creations roaming throughout the Grove... this doesn't bode well. In this calamity, the Grove's fate looks grim. After reporting to Aglaea, (Trailblazer)'s group continues deeper into the Grove to investigate.*

**Trianne:** Everything will be fine! The people at the Grove are really smart. They'll definitely be able to chase away the black tide!

> **缇安：**缇安觉得…会没事的！树庭的大家都很聪明，肯定能赶跑黑潮。

**Mem:** Aglaea said that Anaxa is still alive. If that's true, then there's still hope for everyone else too...

> **迷迷：**而且，阿格莱雅说那位那刻夏还活着，那其他人应该也有希望……

**Mem:** Who is this Anaxa anyway? Why does everybody seem to have some kind of trust in him?

> **迷迷：**…不过这人到底是谁？总觉得大家对他有一种莫名的信任？

**Castorice:** How should I put it...

> **遐蝶：**怎么说呢……

**Castorice:** Lady Aglaea doesn't "trust" Anaxa at all, I'm afraid.

> **遐蝶：**阿格莱雅大人对他的态度，恐怕截然相反吧。

- ▸ He sounds like a big shot.
- ▸ Ah... That makes complete sense...

**Mem:** Hm? Were you in a similar situation before, friend?

> **迷迷：**嗯？好伙伴，你也遇到过什么麻烦吗？

**Castorice:** Believe me, she is far nicer to the two of you than she is to Anaxa.

> **遐蝶：**相信我，阁下。她对两位的态度远远好过那刻夏。

**Castorice:** Despite being a Chrysos Heir, Anaxa resolutely believes that Titans and prophecies are a load of nonsense.

> **遐蝶：**那刻夏老师身为黄金裔…却坚定地认为「泰坦」和「神谕」都是一场笑话。

**Castorice:** During our regular citizens' assemblies, he always votes to oppose the Flame-Chase Journey.

> **遐蝶：**定期召开的公民大会上，对逐火之旅表决的反对票里，也总会有一票出自他之手。

**Mem:** Huh? And he's still able to conduct research here? Didn't you just say that the Grove is an academy that was established around a Titan?

> **迷迷：**啊？这种人还在这里搞研究…你们刚才不是说，树庭是围绕泰坦建立的学院嘛？

**Trianne:** Trianne knows the answer to that! Everybody says that Cerces, who symbolizes reason, is the most tolerant of novel ideas. Apart from Kephale, they're the Titan who treats us the best.

> **缇安：**这个，缇安知道！大家都说，象征理性的瑟希斯，对新思想最宽容了——它是和刻法勒一样的，对我们最最好的泰坦。

**Mem:** Well, it looks like Cerces must really be open-minded if they're accepting of even this kind of novel idea.

> **迷迷：**可连这种*新思想*也…看来这位瑟希斯确实宽容。

**Castorice:** Yes, that is how we formed an agreement with these Titans who bear no harm toward humanity. If they agree to support us on our Flame-Chase Journey, we will wait till the final moments of the journey to restore their Coreflames.

> **遐蝶：**嗯，因此我们和这些对人类抱有善意的泰坦达成协定：在逐火之旅中，它们会为我们提供支持，而我们也会只有在旅途的最后一刻，才会将它们的火种归位。

**Trianne:** ...With the exception of Oronyx, that is. They didn't lend us their power willingly.

> **缇安：**除了…欧洛尼斯。它是不得已才将力量交给我们的。

**Mem:** I don't think Oronyx was as reluctant to help us as you put it, or they wouldn't have deemed (Trailblazer) worthy and allowed me to journey alongside all of you!

> **迷迷：**人家倒是觉得，欧洛尼斯没那么勉为其难。不然它也不可能认可开拓者，还让人家和大家一起旅行啦，对不对？

**Castorice:** That is true. It's also fortunate that these gods weren't corrupted by the black tide and did not descend into madness...

> **遐蝶：**确实。也多亏这几位尊神先前没有受到黑潮影响，陷入疯狂……

**Trianne:** Trianne believes Cerces will be alright! After all, everybody says that the people of the Grove are really smart.

> **缇安：**缇安相信，瑟希斯不会有事的！毕竟都说了，树庭的大家都很聪明嘛。

**Trianne:** So, Little Gray and Little Cas, just leave the task of finding Anaxa and everyone else to Trianne! It's easy for Trianne to look for people because we can fly!

> **缇安：**所以，小小灰，小小蝶，寻找小那刻夏和大家的任务，就交给缇安吧——缇安找人可厉害了，可以飞上天！

**Castorice:** Good idea. In that case, please bring the Weft along with you, Lady Trianne. The golden thread will be much more effective from up above.

> **遐蝶：**也好。那缇安大人带上「引纬」吧，金丝在高处应该能发挥更大作用。

**Castorice:** Please promise us that you'll take care of yourself.

> **遐蝶：**不过，请千万答应我们…保护好自己。

**Trianne:** Okie-dokie! If Trianne gets in trouble, we'll look for all of you. In return, if you get in trouble, remember to call for Trianne, okay? We'll come right over and open the Gate for you!

> **缇安：**好！缇安遇到危险，一定会来找大家的。当然，如果大家遇到危险，也要记得呼唤缇安——缇安会赶来给大家开门！

**Castorice:** That's the other thing I wish to mention: Do not open the Gate unless you have no other choice, Lady Trianne. Your body is... not in good shape at the moment.

> **遐蝶：**这就是我要说的第二件事了：不到万不得已，千万不要「开门」。你的身体状况并不乐观。

**Trianne:** Ah... Okay, we will keep that in mind. Trianne was just trying to help...

> **缇安：**唔，*我们*记得的。缇安只是想帮大家……

**Castorice:** Of course we know that. After all, Lady Trianne is a demigod, the most powerful among all of us here.

> **遐蝶：**我们当然知道啦，毕竟缇安大人可是我们之中最最厉害的「半神」。

**Castorice:** Remember all those brave heroes in the tales you've heard? They would always save their strength until the very last moment. That way, they could ensure that nothing went wrong with their plans. Isn't that right?

> **遐蝶：**还记得故事里勇敢的大英雄吗？他们都会把强大的力量留到最后再用，这样才能万无一失，对不对？

**Trianne:** Mm-hmm! In that case, brave Trianne shall clear the way for all of you!

> **缇安：**小小蝶，说得对！那，勇敢的缇安，先给大家开路！

**Mem:** Little Trianne is so motivated... I mustn't fall behind!

> **迷迷：**小缇安真有干劲…人家也不能被落下哪。

**Mem:** I'll use something to help all of my friends that only I have: Oronyx's power!

> **迷迷：**就用人家才有的，欧洛尼斯的力量，来帮各位好伙伴的忙！

### Open the gate and continue forward

**Mem:** I wonder if I'll find any memories.

> **迷迷：**嗅嗅…会有记忆的气息吗？

**Castorice:** So many monsters...

> **遐蝶：**这么多怪物……

**Mem:** Maybe I'll find some useful memories here... Let me try.

> **迷迷：**或许能找到有价值的记忆——人家试试看。

*(Investigate the monster on the ground, optional)*

The monster's body is twisted like brambles, not allowing any living being to approach.

**Mem:** Let me see...

> **迷迷：**让人家来看看……

**Mem:** Ow, that hurt! And I didn't even detect any memories...

> **迷迷：**啊…扎到人家了！而且什么都没有……

*(Investigate the monster on the stairs, optional)*

The black-colored monster has fallen like a toppled statue. Within its body lie golden ashes, perhaps the same substance as the golden blood that is commonly seen in Amphoreus.

**Mem:** Mem... Memem...

> **迷迷：**迷…迷迷……

**Mem:** It's no good... I didn't see anything.

> **迷迷：**不行…人家什么也没看到。

*(Investigate the monster on the balcony, optional)*

A despicable, twisted life form — it was once a living soul that could mourn pain, but now it can only turn its head at others, until its soul drifts silently out of its shell.

**Mem:** Maybe this monster will show us some useful memories...

> **迷迷：**这只怪物或许承载了有用的记忆……

**Mem:** ...

> **迷迷：**……

**Mem:** I shouldn't have gotten my hopes up.

> **迷迷：**好吧…是人家想多了。

*(Once all three monsters have been investigated)*

**Mem:** Ugh, I really tried my hardest... Why couldn't I see even a sliver of a memory?

> **迷迷：**呜，人家尽力了…怎么一点点记忆都看不到呢？

**Castorice:** It could be because when a life is devoured by the black tide... it loses its soul.

> **遐蝶：**或许是因为…被黑潮吞噬的生命，已毫无灵魂可言了吧。

*(Investigate the gate)*

**(Trailblazer):** (Hm, it's locked...)

> **开拓者：**（嗯？锁住了……）

**Castorice:** I can't open it... It was probably sealed in order to ward off the black tide.

> **遐蝶：**打不开…是为了抵御黑潮，把门封上了吧。

- ▸ There's no keyhole in this door...

**Castorice:** Yes, most of the doors on Amphoreus are sealed using the power of Janus.

> **遐蝶：**嗯，翁法罗斯的绝大多数门都是靠雅努斯的力量封闭的。

- ▸ Flaming lance, chaaaarge!

**Castorice:** Save your energy. Janus's power... is far stronger than we could ever imagine.

> **遐蝶：**还是不必费力了，阁下。雅努斯的力量…远比我们想象的更强韧。

**Mem:** Since this door is a creation of the Three Titans of Fate, maybe the eyes of Oronyx will help me find a way to open it...

> **迷迷：**既然是命运三泰坦的造物，那说不定能借欧洛尼斯的眼睛找到窍门……

**???:** Are you serious? Just a mere door, and your first instinct is to call on the gods?

> **？？？：**笑话。一扇门而已，第一时间竟想的是诉诸众神……

**Mem:** Memi...!?

> **迷迷：**迷…？！

**???:** Before you try unlocking the door, perhaps you should first find a way to clear the fog that clouds your vision.

> **？？？：**打开门上的锁前，还是先设法打开眼上的锁吧。

**Mem:** You scared me! Are you... the echoes of a memory?

> **迷迷：**吓人家一跳！你…是记忆的回声？

**Anaxa:** My name is Anaxagoras, one of the Seven Sages of the Grove of Epiphany and the founder of the Nousporists. Before we go any further: Rule number one, do not call me Anaxa.

> **那刻夏：**我名为阿那克萨戈拉斯，神悟树庭七贤人之一，「智种学派」的创始者。丑话先说在前头：第一，别叫我那刻夏。

**Mem:** Anaxago... Anax... Ugh, I'll just stick to Anaxa. Now, can you tell me—

> **迷迷：**阿那克…那刻…哎，算了，还是叫你那刻夏吧。现在，你能回答……

**Anaxa:** Rule number two: Never interrupt me. Silence is golden.

> **那刻夏：**第二，别打断我——沉默是金。

**Mem:** What a difficult person!

> **迷迷：**这个人，好难相处呀！

**Anaxa:** The person standing here and conversing with you right now isn't the vestiges of a memory. Rather, it is the culmination of the greatness of science and the pure, irrefutable, and undeniable exalted truth.

> **那刻夏：**此刻，站在这里与各位交谈的，并非记忆的残留，而是我科学的结晶，高贵、纯粹、确凿、无疑的真理。

**Anaxa:** Through the wonders of alchemy, I have shattered my soul and transmuted it into gold before burying it in this spot. I have recorded these words so that visitors may learn where their next destination lies.

> **那刻夏：**藉「炼金」之道，我将灵魂打碎，炼成黄金，埋入此地。我录下这些话，以便来人明白自己该去向何处。

**Mem:** Alchemy can do that?

> **迷迷：**「炼金术」还能做到这种事？

**Anaxa:** Nothing is impossible. Having said that, alchemy is not omnipotent. Each time you hear from me, an equivalent part of my soul is being consumed as the price. In other words — this voice isn't created out of thin air.

> **那刻夏：**一切皆有可能，但要注意，炼金并非万全之术。各位所能听到的内容取决于我付出的灵魂当量，并非无中生有。

**Mem:** But how is it that you can respond to me...? Are you really the echoes of a memory?

> **迷迷：**怎么又接住人家的话了…你真是记忆的回声吗？

**Anaxa:** That's because my precise calculations account for every possibility. I also know that you have already discovered the cause of the calamity at the Grove — the black tide — and that you mistakenly believe that the scholars have been wiped.

> **那刻夏：**因为我的计算纤悉无遗。我还知道各位已发现树庭因黑潮遭难，更误以为学者们已经全军覆没。

**Anaxa:** But that is where you're wrong: While the black tide was still approaching the Grove, the sages found a way to evacuate the vast majority of the scholars. They are heading toward the holy city of Okhema as we speak.

> **那刻夏：**但你们大错特错——贤人们已经在黑潮抵近前，设法将绝大部分人疏散。他们此时正在前往圣城奥赫玛的路上。

**Anaxa:** Only a small fraction of the scholars stayed behind with me to defend the Grove. If all goes well, Cerces' Coreflame will reach Okhema before long...

> **那刻夏：**只有小部分学者与我一起留守，捍卫树庭。如果不出意外，瑟希斯的火种应在不久后同时抵达奥赫玛……

**Anaxa:** ...But since you have accessed this message, something unforeseen must have occurred. For now, you should continue onward to the Luminary Throne where you may retrieve the Coreflame... and our mortal remains.

**Castorice:** The riddle-like trials, your distrustful yet sincere character, referring to Mnestia as your love, and...

**Castorice:** The head of the Lotophagists is actually called Medea and not Calypso.

**Cerces:** Since you knew from the start that I was not who I said I was, why did you wait until now to expose me?

> **瑟希斯：**既然汝从初时起便明白吾并非其人，怎到了现在才戳穿？

**Castorice:** Because I did not believe that Cerces would walk this world in a human form. I was also afraid that the Titans were under the control of the black tide and would do us harm...

> **遐蝶：**因为我不相信瑟希斯竟会以人形行走在大地上，也担忧泰坦已遭黑潮挟制，恐将置我们于死地……

**Castorice:** Also, "to make a claim, one must have evidence" — this is the precious knowledge that I acquired during my time at the Grove.

> **遐蝶：**还有，「出言必有据」——这正是我在树庭求学时习得的宝贵知识。

**Cerces:** Heh... Excellent. To think we were able to gain a new chance at life in the midst of death — fate truly is a mysterious thing.

> **瑟希斯：**呵…甚好。吾等竟能于死境中取得如此生机，命运还真是深奥难解哪。

**Castorice:** Well... Shouldn't you also tell us why you went through all the trouble of setting up these riddles?

> **遐蝶：**既然如此…也该告诉我们，您为何要大费周章，设下如此谜题了吧？

**Cerces:** Oh, the reason's quite simple.

> **瑟希斯：**唔，理由很简单哪？

**Cerces:** I needed your help, and just like how you were wary of me, I had to ensure that you were not villains who were trying to get their hands on the Coreflame. I also had to ascertain that you were capable of dealing with the threat at hand.

> **瑟希斯：**吾需要帮助——可就像诸位对吾有所提防一样，吾也须确保，汝等并非妄图染指火种的恶徒，且有能耐对付近在眼前的威胁。

**Castorice:** Is this threat related to the black tide?

> **遐蝶：**这「威胁」与黑潮有关？

**Cerces:** Indeed, and it's even more related to that lost item from before. Its owner is a quiet and paranoid hunter who seems to have been reborn from the black tide and is attacking along with it.

> **瑟希斯：**正是，更与方才那失物有关。其主人乃是沉默且多疑的猎手，似是自黑潮中脱胎，与黑潮一道袭来。

**Cerces:** As I could not do anything about him, I split my body up and escaped to avoid harm. I bided my time for a chance to seek assistance. Unfortunately, this was not a sustainable long-term plan.

> **瑟希斯：**吾实在奈何他不得，只得裂身而逃，以免戕害，迁延日月，伺机求援。可惜，这也并非长久之计。

**Trianne:** Uh... What you mean is that you hid your own Coreflame?

> **缇安：**唔…你的意思是，你把自己的火种藏起来了？

**Cerces:** Indeed, I split it into three. I hid one within the Golden Bough of Vows, and sealed another in the amber along with Mnestia's remaining embers...

> **瑟希斯：**正是，分作了三份。一份藏于「金枝誓言」中，一份与墨涅塔的余火一道封入琥珀……

**Cerces:** As for the last one... It's currently in Anaxa's body, which is in dire need of repair.

> **瑟希斯：**至于那最后一份…如今正在那刻夏的躯壳中，亟待修复呢。

**Castorice:** What? Do you mean that, you're using such a method...

> **遐蝶：**什么？难道说，你是用这种方法……

**Cerces:** Hehe, after all, the scholars of the Grove have always been willing to lay down their lives to defend my Coreflame... As a respected god, it would be too embarrassing if I were to do nothing and simply watch the children of humanity give up their lives for me.

> **瑟希斯：**呵呵，毕竟树庭的学者们为了捍卫吾之火种，不惜赴火蹈刃，从容就义…吾既贵为尊神，若只眼睁睁看着人子们舍己成人，未免太没面子。

**Cerces:** Especially that heretic named Anaxa. He did not even hesitate to tear his soul apart and use it to perform a miracle. He trapped all the creations of the black tide within the Grove so that they would not be able to harm others.

> **瑟希斯：**尤其是那名唤那刻夏的异端——不惜磔裂灵魂当作础石，引发奇迹，在这树庭中布下天罗地网，将一众黑潮造物困于樊笼中，以免殃及他方。

**Cerces:** His way of thinking was so out of the ordinary that I felt it would be a waste for him to meet his demise in the black tide. And so, I decided to save his life.

> **瑟希斯：**吾觉着这道思想如此奇异，随黑潮白白消散实属可惜，便出手救了人子一命。

**Cerces:** Of course, this also meant that I was able to conceal myself and remain hidden... Well, that's also an "equivalent exchange," right?

> **瑟希斯：**当然，吾也得以匿身潜藏…嗯，这也是「等价交换」的一道侧面吧？

**Mem:** Ah, I finally understand! So, you want us to lend you a hand in piecing your Coreflame back together! We'll also be able to save Anaxa that way!

> **万敌：**…我没看走眼吧？了不起的「救世主」，终于愿意出门见光了？

**Phainon:** Hmph.

> **白厄：**哼。

**Mydei:** "Hmph"...? What kind of response is that?

> **万敌：**「哼」…这算哪门子回答？

**Phainon:** Nothing. I was just imitating your usual response.

> **白厄：**没什么——模仿你平时的样子罢了。

**Phainon:** I don't feel anything special from you... Have you passed the demigod trial?

> **白厄：**我没在你身上感受到特别的气息…半神的考验，你通过了么？

**Mydei:** The ritual hasn't reopened yet. In case you forgot — It hasn't been THAT long since I pulled you out of the trial.

> **万敌：**仪式还未重启。免得你忘记——距离我将你揪出试炼才没过多久。

**Phainon:** I didn't forget.

> **白厄：**我没忘。

**Mydei:** Then, you're welcome.

> **万敌：**不用谢。

**Phainon:** "In twilight's last gleam, Okhema stands bold. Her walls sturdy, tales untold."

> **白厄：**「…长夜最后的光亮，城垣坚固的奥赫玛。」

**Phainon:** "Dreaming of her fruitful gardens, pilgrims arrive from distant lands. Figs and olives, O so grand..."

> **白厄：**「有多少人远道而来，以为她是常有无花果和橄榄的丰美园林……」

**Phainon:** "...Yet little do they know, that they will step into flames, fuel to the fire, a burning brand."

> **白厄：**「…却不曾想，跌入了燃烧的炉膛？」

**Mydei:** What are you playing at this time?

> **万敌：**你又在耍什么把戏？

**Phainon:** I just felt like reciting a few lines of poetry. Surely the crown prince of Kremnos knows how to appreciate poetry, right?

> **白厄：**一时来了兴致，想念几句诗而已。悬锋的王子，不会听不懂诗歌吧？

**Mydei:** Kremnoans steer clear of such weak literature, but my library doesn't lack collections of this kind —

> **万敌：**悬锋人不读这种软弱的文字，但我的图书馆里不乏这类藏书——

**Phainon:** These lines express the poet's yearning for their home, just as Okhema is distant from many people's homes.

> **白厄：**这几句表达了作者的思乡之情，正如奥赫玛离大部分人的故乡都很遥远。

**Mydei:** ...I know how to interpret poetry, HKS.

> **万敌：**——我听得懂诗，HKS。

**Phainon:** Ha... There's no point in pretending to be profound, my friend. Thank you for helping me out when I was in a tight spot, but it seems to me that you aren't prepared for the trial either.

> **白厄：**哈…别装深沉了，朋友。感谢你在危机关头拉了我一把，但依我看，你也没做好试炼的准备吧？

**Mydei:** Don't compare your situation with mine. My people are still relying on me. I can't ascend to godhood on my own without first resolving their issues.

> **万敌：**别把你我的处境混为一谈。我背后还有族人，在解决他们的问题前，我不能独自登神。

**Phainon:** So... are you suggesting that you, the mighty Mydeimos and the heir to the throne of Kremnos, wish to defy the tradition that has endured among your people for millennia?

> **白厄：**嚯…听你的意思，勇猛的迈德漠斯，悬锋王位的继承人，居然想打破一族延续千年的古老传统？

**Mydei:** ...

> **万敌：**……

**Mydei:** I'm still thinking about what I should do.

> **万敌：**该怎么做，我仍在思考。

> **白厄：**算了，恐怕还轮不到我来给你提建议。

**Phainon:** An idea does strike me, though...

> **白厄：**不过，我倒是突然冒出一个想法……

**Mydei:** Go ahead. Let's see what terrible ideas you've come up with this time.

> **万敌：**讲吧，又是什么馊主意？

**Phainon:** Maybe we both need to relieve some stress. Come, Mydeimos! Let's recommence our previous competition...

> **白厄：**或许我们都需要释放压力。来吧，迈德漠斯，延续你我之前的竞赛……

**Phainon:** This time... We'll see who can stay in the super hot bath for longer!

> **白厄：**这次——就比比谁能在*超高温*浴池里坚持更久！

**Mydei:** ...

> **万敌：**……

**Phainon:** What? Are you chickening out?

> **白厄：**怎么，怯场了？

> **万敌：**悬锋人的字典里没有「怯场」二字。我只是觉得可笑。野史学家，你难道没听说过悬锋人沐浴，用的都是锻剑淬铁的滚烫铁水？

**Mydei:** Looks like I'll have to teach you some royal etiquette today!

> **万敌：**今日，我就来教你这些宫廷礼数！

**Bath Sprite:** And thus, an unprecedented sauna battle commences.

> **浴场精灵：**那二人的决心登峰造极，强烈的意志令水汽都为之色变，气焰裹挟着水汽直冲云霄，遮天蔽日，使人目不能视……

> **浴场精灵：**还记得某位老战士提及的，「歌耳戈猎狮」的传说吗？故事的主人…主狮公，其实并非一方之霸，还有一头剑羚能与之分庭抗礼。

**Bath Sprite:** Long before the dawn of Era Chrysea, the two beasts started their timeless battle on the plains of Tretos. The lion regarded the oryx as its food, while the oryx perceived the lion as a scornful, oppressive, and challenging barrier that it swore to topple with its antlers...

> **浴场精灵：**早在黄金世前，两头野兽便已在特雷托斯平原上捉对厮杀。雄狮当剑羚作盘中餐，而剑羚视之为一堵蔑视、压迫、磨难的高墙，它誓用犄角将其推翻……

**Bath Sprite:** For tens of thousands of years, without respite, the two creatures thus continued their relentless pursuit and rivalry...

> **浴场精灵：**于是，数万年如一日地，两头野兽在荒野中开始了角逐和追逃——

> **万敌：**不自量力地向悬锋人发出挑战，这就是下场。

**Aglaea:** What I'm saying is... Your little game dragged down quite a few innocent souls alongside you.

> **阿格莱雅：**我是说…你们的小小游戏，可是连带着让不少人一起遭了殃呢。

> **万敌：**…那些被杀气震晕的浴客，我们会送他们离开的。

> **白厄：**这…不公平……

> **万敌：**别用那眼神盯着我…是他下的战书。悬锋的字典里没有「逃避」二字。

**Aglaea:** Very well, let us bring this farce to a close, shall we? Warriors, it is time to get moving.

After recovery, Phainon and Mydei escorted the fainted bathers away from the scene.

**Hyacine:** Such an empty bath is indeed a rare sight...

> **风堇：**这么空旷的浴场是难得一见……

**Hyacine:** I was looking forward to unwinding after work. I'd be delighted to accept your invitation, Lady Tribbie!

### Take a bath

*(Approach the marked location)*

**Hyacine:** Let me check... The water temperature feels just perfect!

> **风堇：**让我先试试…水温刚好呢！

**Aglaea:** So, how is the feeling of having the entire bath to yourselves?

**Tribbie:** Heehee, it's sooo good...

> **缇宝：**嘿嘿，太舒服啦……

**Hyacine:** But, Lady Tribbie... Aren't you going to soak in the bath with us?

> **风堇：**不过，缇宝老师…你不一起泡泡么？

**Tribbie:** We will have to pass. After all, we*ve become children.

> **缇宝：** *我们*就不啦，毕竟变成小孩子了嘛。

**Aglaea:** Please understand that she can no longer endure the heat of this pool due to the blessings of Janus.

> **阿格莱雅：**请见谅。受雅努斯的赐福影响，她已不能耐受这池子的水温了。

**Hyacine:** Oh...

> **风堇：**唔……

**Tribbie:** Are you curious about us, Hyacine?

> **缇宝：**你是对*我们*的事感到好奇吗，小风堇？

**Hyacine:** I am, but something tells me... that it might be better to change the topic?

> **风堇：**是有些好奇啦，但直觉告诉我…是不是换个话题比较好？

**Tribbie:** Don't get all nervous. It's not something we can't discuss! The truth is... a long, long time ago... we were a mature adult just like Agy.

> **缇宝：**紧张什么呀，不是不能说的事！其实呢，*我们*在很久很久以前…也像阿雅一样，是成熟的大人哦。

**Tribbie:** Then, because of reasons... we inherited Janus' divinity and gained the power of "Passage"...

> **缇宝：**但因为种种原因，*我们*接过雅努斯的神职，得到了「门径」的力量……

**Tribbie:** And in return, the price we had to pay was the splitting of our soul.

> **缇宝：**而作为交换，付出的便是灵魂分裂的代价。

**Aglaea:** Each opening of the Century Gate intensifies the strain on Tribbie and the others. They are gradually regressing towards infancy, both in body and mind.

> **阿格莱雅：**每一次洞开「百界门」，都会深化分裂对缇宝她们的影响。从肉体到心智，一步步向着婴儿退行。

**Tribbie:** We don't know what awaits us at the end, either. But anyway, that's why we have turned into children.

> **缇宝：**其实*我们*也不知道终点会是什么样子。总而言之，这就是*我们*变成小孩子的原因。

**Hyacine:** In that case... when you opened that giant Century Gate above Okhema during the battle against Nikador...

> **风堇：**那…先前和尼卡多利对决时，你们在奥赫玛上空张开的巨大「百界门」，岂不是……

**Tribbie:** Well, there's no way around it. We all know that the Flame-Chase Journey won't be a smooth ride.

> **缇宝：**没办法，逐火之旅注定不会一帆风顺嘛。

**Hyacine:** ...

> **风堇：**……

**Hyacine:** Maybe... We can find a way to fix it. The primary research focus of Nousporists is the soul. We explore its depletion and methods to restore dispersed souls... As long as research continues, there might be a breakthrough that can help.

> **风堇：**也许…会有办法的。智种学派的核心课题就是灵魂，针对它的损耗，或是弥补已经消散的灵魂…只要研究继续下去，没准会有新的发现。

**Hyacine:** We will do everything we can.

> **风堇：**我们会尽力而为。

**Aglaea:** So, your first reaction is in fact denial? You're indeed Anaxa's student.

> **阿格莱雅：**第一反应竟然是否定么？不愧是那刻夏的门生。

**Aglaea:** I look forward to your good news, Hyacine.

> **阿格莱雅：**我期待你的好消息，风堇。

**Tribbie:** Haha, now there's another reason to look forward to tomorrow...

> **缇宝：**好像…不太对劲，强烈的感应又来了…是缇安？

> **缇宝：**不、不对…这次不是缇安……

### Observe Phainon conversing with Mydei

> *Following a ridiculous contest, Phainon and Mydei stop to rest. The two warriors discuss the Coreflame trial, revealing untold stories from their pasts...*

In the meantime...

**Phainon:** Ha, I win this round of escorting the bathers home.

> **白厄：**哈，这次护送浴客是我赢了。

**Mydei:** How does one contender alone make a valid competition?

> **万敌：**单方面开启的竞赛，如何能够作数？

**Phainon:** Offering a compliment or two wouldn't be the end of you, you know.
