**Mydei:** ...HKS.

> **万敌：**…HKS。

**Phainon:** Hai... What is that word? Is it your native tongue?

> **白厄：**海乙…你在说什么，家乡话？

**Mydei:** It means "hyena" — I'm likening you to a weak hyena. By the way, you emphasized the completely wrong syllable.

> **万敌：**「鬣狗」——我在将你比作一条瘦弱的鬣狗。顺带一提，你的重音完全不对。

**Phainon:** Though we've been acquainted for some time, I have... rarely heard you talk in the ancient tongue of Kremnos.

> **白厄：**咱俩认识也有段时间了，听你说悬锋古语的次数…屈指可数啊。

**Mydei:** It's because I'm not exactly a master at it, either. I can listen and read just fine, but when it comes to writing...

> **万敌：**因为我也不擅长这门语言。听和读不成问题。但若要让我以古语写作……

**Phainon:** You can write?

> **白厄：**你还会写东西？

**Mydei:** You speak as if you don't have a brain.

> **万敌：**你的发言仿佛没有脑子。

**Phainon:** Haha, that was in jest. I understand. After all, you... spent all your childhood wandering far from home.

> **白厄：**哈哈，开玩笑的。也难怪，毕竟你童年一直在…漂泊，离家很远。

**Mydei:** Worry about yourself first, Deliverer. You also never mention your hometown apart from when you need to introduce yourself.

> **万敌：**多关心下你自己吧，救世主。除去自报家门的场合，你也从不提及故乡。

**Phainon:** Well... It's because there isn't much to say. Aedes Elysiae is simply a tiny village that disappeared many years ago in the flames of war.

> **白厄：**呵…因为实在没什么可说的。哀丽秘榭只是个小村庄，因为战火，早就不复存在了。

**Phainon:** The only times I get to see it are in my dreams... and in...

> **白厄：**我唯一能见到它的场合就是在梦中。还有……

**Mydei:** And in?

> **万敌：**还有？

**Phainon:** ...And in the trial from the Lance of Fury.

> **白厄：**…在「天谴之矛」的试炼里。

**Mydei:** I knew it.

> **万敌：**果然啊。

**Phainon:** Yes, I saw it all. Aedes Elysiae ablaze, with everyone — my family, friends, and my kin — lying in a sea of flames.

> **白厄：**嗯，我看见了，燃烧的哀丽秘榭，我的亲人、朋友、同族…所有人都倒在火海里。

**Phainon:** A blood-red half-sun hung in the sky, just like on that day. And before my eyes, that murderer...

> **白厄：**天上挂着半轮血日，就和那时一样，那个凶手在我面前……

**Phainon:** ...killed Cyrene.

> **白厄：**…杀死了昔涟。

**Phainon:** But I saw its form this time... A black cloak, an eerie mask, and a broken giant sword that carried an ominous aura...

> **白厄：**不过这一次，我看清了它的样貌：黑色的斗篷，诡异的面具。破碎的、散发着不详气息的巨剑……

**Phainon:** I fought it in the trial. But even though I've been fighting, improving, and resolutely getting stronger all this time... I still couldn't defeat it.

> **白厄：**在试炼里，我和那家伙交手了。但过去这么久，即便我带着决心一路战斗、成长、变强…我还是战胜不了它。

**Mydei:** You were blinded by hatred and almost lost yourself in that trial.

> **万敌：**仇恨蒙蔽了你的双眼，你差点就要迷失在那试炼里了。

**Phainon:** That's right... And that's why I am truly thankful to you, Mydeimos.

> **白厄：**对…所以我由衷地感谢你，迈德漠斯。

**Mydei:** Hmph, you've finally picked up some manners. Better late than never.

> **万敌：**哼，总算学会礼仪了，倒也不算太晚。

**Phainon:** Then what about you, Mydei? What did you see in that trial? ...What do you fear?

> **白厄：**那你呢，万敌？你在那试炼里看见了什么…你的恐惧又是什么？

**Mydei:** There is no word for "fear" in the Kremnoan language.

> **万敌：**首先，悬锋人的字典里没有「恐惧」二字。

**Phainon:** ...I'm getting the feeling that your language has no words at all.

> **白厄：**…你们的字典里是不是压根没字？

**Mydei:** But to answer your question, I saw Okhema in the trial, as well as...

> **万敌：**然后，我在其中看到了奥赫玛。以及……

**Mydei:** ...My old comrades.

> **万敌：**…我曾经的战友们。

**Mydei:** Hephaestion, Perdikkas, Leonnius, Ptolemy, and Peucesta... Ever since I returned from the Sea of Souls, these five had been by my side.

> **万敌：**赫菲斯辛、帕狄卡斯、莱昂、托勒密，还有朴塞塔…自我从冥海归来，那五人便始终拱卫左右。

**Mydei:** We faced life and death together during our days in exile. Those ten years were arduous, yet filled with moments worth treasuring. Each night, we would gather by the campfire, savoring honey brews and singing our hearts out...

> **万敌：**流亡的岁月，他们与我同生共死。那十年时光，颠沛流离，但也值得怀念。每个夜晚，我们都会在营火边围坐，畅饮蜜酿，纵情高歌……

**Mydei:** The wind in the wilds of Amphoreus smells like rust. Hephaestion would always tease me about adding goat milk to the honey brew, claiming I ruined the blood-red color of the drink. He had a scrawny figure, but was a relentless force on the battlefield, dispatching foes without so much as a blink.

> **万敌：**翁法罗斯的野风有铁锈的味道。赫菲斯辛总笑话我喝蜜酿加羊奶，说把鲜红的血色拌得不伦不类。他生得瘦小，但在战场上凶悍无比，杀敌如麻。

**Mydei:** Perdikkas was an expert in concocting medicines, delving into all those strange folk remedies. Leonnius was a swift runner and our most reliable messenger.

> **万敌：**帕狄卡斯精通制药，净整些偏方怪方；莱昂善于奔跑，是最受信赖的信使。

**Mydei:** Ptolemy was a real bookworm, always spouting off in chapters and verses. When we left Kremnos, he made off with a stack of ancient tomes from the grand library — not like it mattered, since my family owns the library. And then there was Peucesta... Heh, he was the silent type, but a truly masterful musician...

> **万敌：**托勒密总爱咬文嚼字，离开悬锋时，从大图书馆顺走了不少古籍——都是我家的，也不用还了。至于朴塞塔…呵，他不爱说话，但弹得一手好琴……

**Phainon:** ...This is the first time I've heard you mention these five and their names.

> **白厄：**这五位…我还是第一次听说他们的名字。

**Mydei:** They died on the battlefield before the Kremnoan detachment managed to join Okhema.

> **万敌：**当然。在悬锋孤军与奥赫玛联合前，他们就战死了。

**Phainon:** ...

> **白厄：**……

**Mydei:** Perdikkas was the first to go. He was shot in the stomach by a poisonous arrow from the people of Ladon in the second Month of Cultivation. In the end, his remedies mended all but himself.

> **万敌：**先是帕狄卡斯，他死在第二个耕耘月，被拉冬人的毒箭射中腹部。他的药方能救所有人，唯独没救下自己。

**Perdikkas:** "Mydeimos, do not mourn. I have achieved honor by bestowing a noble death upon the enemy..."

> **帕狄卡斯：**「迈德漠斯，不要悲伤。我已挣得荣耀，用光荣的死亡回敬夙敌……」

**Mydei:** Leonnius died in the fifth winter. Aidonia's snowstorm lasted three full months. On the night of the fiercest storm, our foes launched their assault.

> **万敌：**莱昂死在第五个冬天，哀地里亚的暴风雪持续了整整三个月。风雪最大的那夜，敌人发动了袭击。

**Phainon:** Aidonia... Is that the city Castorice used to live in?

> **白厄：**哀地里亚…是遐蝶以前所在的城邦？

**Mydei:** Yes. Those zealots were as fearless as Kremnoans in the face of death, but they lacked respect for honor.

> **万敌：**就是那群狂信徒，他们如悬锋人一般视死如生，唯独少了对荣誉的敬畏。

**Mydei:** They cornered us in a bottomless swamp. Leonnius covered for our retreat, and they cut his legs off. He vanished into the mire of a foreign land, his body never to be recovered.

> **万敌：**他们在无底沼泽中围追堵截，莱昂为了殿后被斩断双腿。他消失在异乡的泥土里，尸骨无存。

**Leonnius:** "This is perfect, Mydeimos... I can finally deliver the news of you ascending the throne to the nether realm..."

> **莱昂：**「多好啊，迈德漠斯…我终于能将你成王的讯息带向冥界了……」

**Mydei:** Twenty dusks later, we finally breached the walls of Aidonia and entertained the Usurper's plea for peace. However, the rebels refused to surrender and set up an ambush within the city...

> **万敌：**那之后的第二十个黄昏，我们终于反攻入哀地里亚城中，接受了僭主的求和，但负隅顽抗的叛军却在城中设伏……

**Mydei:** Ptolemy and his cavalry met their end before they could even relish our triumph. Those obelisks that collapsed after being set on fire became their final resting place.

> **万敌：**托勒密和他的骑兵队，还未来得及品尝胜利便全军覆没。燃烧崩塌的方尖碑林成为了他们的坟丘。

**Ptolemy:** "Mydeimos, please tell my family: It was not cunning schemes that vanquished Ptolemy, but the immense weight of Amphoreus' entire history."

> **托勒密：**「迈德漠斯，请你转告我的家人：打倒我托勒密的并非歹毒的诡计，而是翁法罗斯一切历史的重量。」

**Mydei:** Then, it was Peucesta. He lured the enemies into the city of Aenionus with his melodies, buying us invaluable time.

> **万敌：**再后来是朴塞塔。他在厄涅俄努斯城中以歌声诱敌深入，为我们争取了宝贵的时间。

**Mydei:** Our enemies, consumed by indignation when they discovered the trick, set the entire city and my soldiers on fire... His final words were recorded on a small clay tablet, fractured by the flames.

> **万敌：**恼羞成怒的敌军不惜点燃大火，将我军和整座城池一道焚烧殆尽…他的遗言只留在一小块被烈火烧裂的泥板上。

**Peucesta:** "Mydeimos, don the crown."

> **朴塞塔：**「迈德漠斯，戴上王冠吧。」

**Mydei:** And at last, my most trusted friend, Hephaestion... He was supposed to stand by my side through it all, but a sudden relapse of his illness hit him on the eve of my duel against my father...

> **万敌：**最后，我最信赖的赫菲斯辛…他本该与我一道见证尘埃落定，却在我与父亲角斗的前夕宿疾复发……

**Mydei:** He hid his condition from me, and by the time I returned victorious, he had but a breath left in him.

> **万敌：**他隐瞒了病情，待我手刃仇敌凯旋，他已只剩一丝气息。

**Hephaestion:** "A Kremnoan dying on a sickbed... I would be a laughingstock to everyone."

> **赫菲斯辛：**「身为悬锋人，却只能在床榻上了却一生…说出去要教人笑话啊。」

**Mydei:** "Hephaestion, my dear friend... Spare your strength. I'll go get a physician..."

> **万敌：**「赫菲斯辛，我的挚友…省些力气罢，我去叫医师来……」

**Hephaestion:** "No need... Even if Perdikkas were here, he couldn't stop destiny from taking me away."

> **赫菲斯辛：**「不必了…就算帕狄卡斯还在这里，也无法替我挡下命运。」

**Mydei:** "No! None can take you away!"

> **万敌：**「没有人能夺走你的命运！」

**Hephaestion:** "Mydeimos... Our king... Do not shed tears for me. It's not befitting of your status."

> **赫菲斯辛：**「迈德漠斯…我们的王啊…不要落泪，那不称你的身份。」

...

**Hephaestion:** "Farewell, my dear friend... You must... lead us back home..."

> **赫菲斯辛：**「别了，挚友。你一定要…带领我等还乡……」

*(Dialogue continues into the next step)*

### Observe Tribbie interrupting Phainon and Mydei's conversation

*(Dialogue continues from the previous step)*

**Phainon:** ...What a grave trial indeed.

> **白厄：**真是…沉重的试炼啊。

**Phainon:** Now I understand why you see "immortality" as a defect... or even a curse.

> **白厄：**我现在能理解，你为何会把「不死」视作一种缺陷，甚至诅咒了。

**Mydei:** My father threw me into the Sea of Souls to break the wheel of karma, but little did he realize it would grant me an immortal body and summon a fated revenge on his head.

> **万敌：**先王为断绝宿业，将我投入冥海，却没料到此举反造就了我的不死之躯，给他招来了命运的复仇。

**Mydei:** Following his demise, I considered leading my people back to our homeland, but Kremnos had already turned into a wasteland. Only darkness and insanity thrived there.

> **万敌：**他死后，我本想过带领族人回归故乡。但彼时的悬锋已是一片荒芜，能在其中生长的只有黑暗与疯狂。

**Mydei:** Against the dissent of many — Krateros included — I made the Kremnoan detachment stay in Okhema, but I know that their glory will never be doused out.

> **万敌：**顶着许多人——也包括克拉特鲁斯——的反对之声，我让悬锋孤军留在了奥赫玛。但我明白，他们的荣耀不可能被磨灭。

**Mydei:** ...The instant of "Strife" shall inevitably arrive.

> **万敌：**…「纷争」的时刻终究会到来。

**Phainon:** If you take over Nikador, then you must begin the journey back to your homeland.

> **白厄：**只要你接过尼卡多利，就必须踏上还乡的旅程。

**Phainon:** So... You hesitate because you think leaving your people in the holy city is a better choice?

> **白厄：**所以，你觉得让族人留在圣城才是更好的选择？

**Mydei:** ...

> **万敌：**……

**Mydei:** I don't know.

> **万敌：**我不知道。

**Mydei:** But I have never been crowned, and those five were never endowed with any titles... None is aware of their glory.

> **万敌：**但因为我从未加冕为王，那五人从未被册封…也无人知晓他们的荣耀。

**Phainon:** ...Your predicament is truly complicated.

> **白厄：**…真是复杂啊，你的处境。

**Phainon:** I don't know how I can give you any useful advice. Maybe... you could seek counsel from more people, such as the Grand Craftsman? Chartonus' people seem to have been through comparable dilemmas.

> **白厄：**我很难给出有用的建议，或许…你可以找更多人谈谈，比如大工匠。哈托努斯的族人应该经历过相似的困境。

**Phainon:** And that teacher of yours, Krateros...

> **白厄：**还有你的那位老师，克拉特鲁斯……

**Tribbie:** De! We've got a big problem! Big problem!!!

> **缇宝：**小敌——大事不妙、不妙啦！

**Phainon:** Lady Tribbie? What's making you so flustered?

> **白厄：**缇宝老师？发生了什么，竟然让你如此惊慌？

**Tribbie:** Hurry up and go save Trinnon! She... She...

> **缇宝：**快去救人，缇宁她，缇宁她……

**Tribbie:** ...She got abducted by Krateros!

> **缇宝：**——被克拉特鲁斯掳走了！

*(Dialogue continues into the next step)*

### Speak with Krateros

> *Krateros' stubborn persistence once again brings the fate and future of the Kremnoan people before you...*

*(Dialogue continues from the previous step)*

**Mydei:** ...Krateros! What's going on?

> **万敌：**…克拉特鲁斯！这是怎么回事？

**Krateros:** Ah, Mydeimos...

> **克拉特鲁斯：**啊，迈德漠斯……

**Krateros:** It seems I was the one who exposed my vulnerabilities first... Heh.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**没想到，是我先暴露了软肋…呵。

**Mydei:** Aglaea! I demand an explanation.

> **万敌：**阿格莱雅！给我一个解释。

**Aglaea:** This man abducted Trinnon by force and forced her to bring him to the Vortex of Genesis.

> **阿格莱雅：**这个男人以武力挟持了缇宁，强迫她带自己来到创世涡心。

**Aglaea:** He then brazenly stepped into the trial of the Lance of Fury... And naturally, failed.

> **阿格莱雅：**然后，他竟然还大胆踏入了「天谴之矛」的试炼…当然，以失败告终。

**Mydei:** Is she telling the truth? Why did you do that?

> **万敌：**她说的是真的吗？你为何要这么做？

**Krateros:** Because I don't want to wait anymore. Your hesitation made me desperate.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**因为我不想再等待了，你的犹疑令人绝望。

**Krateros:** If you want to run away from your destiny to become king, then keep running. Someone will eventually stand up and fill in the gap left by Strife... Then they will lead our people home.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**你若想逃避成王的宿命，那便逃吧。总有人会站出来，填补「纷争」的空缺…引领我族还乡。

**Mydei:** Age has blinded you, teacher!

> **万敌：**吾师，岁月怎会让你变得如此蒙昧！

**Mydei:** Can't you see that I'm hesitating because of this very recklessness seared into your blood?

> **万敌：**你还没看清吗？令我彷徨的理由，正是你这溶入骨血的鲁莽和冲动。

**Aglaea:** Krateros, do you truly believe you're worthy of entering the trial? To blaspheme against the prophecy?

> **阿格莱雅：**克拉特鲁斯，你当真觉得自己有资格染指试炼，亵渎神谕？

**Aglaea:** Does the mission we Chrysos Heirs bear seem like child's play to you?

> **阿格莱雅：**我等黄金裔的使命，在你眼中就是这般儿戏？

**Krateros:** Do you know how the Kremnoans see you, Aglaea?

> **克拉特鲁斯：**想知道你在悬锋人眼里是什么模样吗，阿格莱雅？

**Krateros:** You're a hypocrite... and a megalomaniac! The prophecy of genesis and the Flame-Chase Journey are just excuses for you to seize power.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**伪善者…野心家！创世的神谕，逐火之旅，不过是你用来攫取权力的说辞罢了。

**Mydei:** Silence, Krateros! Do you even know what you're saying?

> **万敌：**住口，克拉特鲁斯！你知道自己在说什么吗？

**Krateros:** Are you defending this woman, Mydeimos? How many wars did people start because of her and her so-called prophecy? How many cities fell in the past millennium, their people massacring each other?

> **克拉特鲁斯：**你要维护这女人吗，迈德漠斯？过去的千年里，围绕她——还有她们宣扬的神谕，多少战火燃起，多少城邦自相残杀？

**Krateros:** Even if this so-called "genesis" really exists, will there be a place for the Kremnoans in that new world?

> **克拉特鲁斯：**就算所谓的「创世」真的存在，那新世界里会有悬锋族人的一席之地吗？

**Trinnon:** ...

> **缇宁：**……

**Aglaea:** Are you done talking?

> **阿格莱雅：**你的发言结束了吗？

**Aglaea:** By agreement between the Chrysos Heirs and the Council of Elders, those who trespass upon the Vortex of Genesis need not be tried by the Council. I am allowed to pass judgment alone.

> **阿格莱雅：**依照黄金裔与元老院达成的协定，私闯创世涡心之人无需经过议会审判，可由我自行发落。

**Aglaea:** Krateros, your actions are unforgivable. I hereby sentence you...

> **阿格莱雅：**克拉特鲁斯，你的行为无可宽恕。我在此宣判……

**Tribbie:** Agy, wait!

> **缇宝：**阿雅！等一下！

**Tribbie:** ...We have a proposal. Someone has to resolve this conflict between the cities.

> **缇宝：** *我们*…有一个提议。这场城邦之间的矛盾，总得有人站出来解决。

**Trinnon:** Lord Krateros did not use violence to harm us. We can sense that his anxiety stems from his concern for his people's future.

> **缇宁：**克拉特鲁斯阁下并未用暴力伤害*我们*。*我们*能感受到，他的焦虑源于对族人前途的担忧。

**Tribbie:** Please, Aglaea. Hear us out first.

> **缇宝：**拜托了，阿格莱雅，请先听听*我们*的想法。

**Aglaea:** ...

> **阿格莱雅：**……

**Aglaea:** I'm listening.

> **阿格莱雅：**我在听。

**Trinnon:** I'm sorry, Lord Krateros. We were so preoccupied with deciphering the fragments of prophecy that we neglected those of you not favored by its message... Your accusations were harsh but justified.

> **缇宁：**抱歉，克拉特鲁斯阁下。我们终日只顾着解读预言的碎片，却忽视了那些未被神谕青睐的人们…你的指控尽管刺耳，但也合乎情理。

**Trinnon:** We wish to earn your trust, and those of the Kremnoans. I think the best way to do that... is to take you to witness the source of the prophecy for yourself.

> **缇宁：**我们想得到你，还有悬锋族人的信任。我想，最好的办法…就是带你亲眼见证预言的源头。

**Krateros:** ...How are you going to show me something that has no form?

> **克拉特鲁斯：**…你要如何让我看见虚无缥缈之物？

**Trinnon:** You've heard about that outlander, right? He/She has somehow made a connection with Oronyx and can completely replicate scenes from the past.

> **缇宁：**你已听说了那位异乡人的事，对吗？出于某种原因，他/她与欧洛尼斯建立了联系，能完整地复现往日的风景。

**Trinnon:** We will ask for his/her aid. Tomorrow, at the Abyss of Fate in the Temple of Janusopolis... we will show you everything from that distant memory, Krateros.

> **缇宁：** *我们*会请求他/她的帮助。明日，在命运重渊，雅努萨波利斯的神殿…克拉特鲁斯，*我们*愿意为你展示那段久远的记忆，毫无保留。

**Krateros:** ...

> **克拉特鲁斯：**……

**Krateros:** ...A fair proposal. I accept.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**…听上去很公平。我接受这个提议。

**Aglaea:** Until then, you will be stripped of your freedom. That is my one condition and I will brook no argument.

> **阿格莱雅：**但在那之前，你将被剥夺行动的自由。这是我的底线，决无让步的可能。

**Krateros:** Hmph, it is not the first time I've been made someone else's prisoner. Do as you please, "demigod."

> **克拉特鲁斯：**呵，这不是我第一次做别人的阶下囚了。悉听尊便，「半神」。

**Mydei:** Teacher... why did you do this?

> **万敌：**吾师…为何要做到这个地步？

**Krateros:** Forgive me, young master, but I just cannot wait any longer.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**抱歉，少主…我不能再忍耐了。

**Krateros:** Ever since your father died, our people and I have been waiting for a day to revive Kremnos. We placed all of our hopes in you but never received an answer.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**自你的父亲死后，我和族人就一直在等待复兴悬锋的时机。我们将一切希望寄托于你，却迟迟没有得到回应。

**Mydei:** This obsession for returning home has become a sickness for our people! Wise as you are, I refuse to believe that you can't see the truth...

> **万敌：**还乡的执念已成了我族的病根！你如此睿智，我不相信你看不清简单的真相……

**Krateros:** You have a clear head, which is why you're also incomparably alone. A smart person can only choose to tangle himself with the mundane of this world.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**你很清醒，因此才无比孤独。聪明的人，只能选择与世俗沉沦。

**Krateros:** If you're truly capable of leading our people beyond this bloodstained fate, then prove it to me once more, Mydeimos.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**你若真能带领族人打破这染血的命运，就再次证明给我看吧，迈德漠斯。

**Krateros:** Just as you did back then...

> **克拉特鲁斯：**就像你当时做到的那样……

**Mydei:** ...

> **万敌：**……

**Old King Eurypon:** A fierce fighter... I did not expect such heroism from a feeble Okheman.

> **先王欧利庞：**真是勇猛的斗士…软弱的奥赫玛人，竟也拥有如此豪杰。

**Old King Eurypon:** Fulfill your duty as the victor, warrior, and tell me your name! I seek an honorable death from you!

> **先王欧利庞：**履行胜者的义务，报上名来，战士！我向你索求荣耀的死亡！

**Mydei:** As if you have any honor!

> **万敌：**「荣耀」…你与这二字何干？

**Mydei:** Listen closely! I am the leader of the Kremnoan detachment — Mydeimos!

> **万敌：**洗耳恭听吧！我是悬锋孤军之首，迈德漠斯——

**Mydei:** I'm here to finish you, father.

> **万敌：**——我来贯穿你的胸膛了，父亲！

**Old King Eurypon:** ...

> **先王欧利庞：**……

**Old King Eurypon:** It is you... the beast spurned by all. So, you did defy death. The prophecy was true... You will bring an end to my Kremnoan dynasty.

> **先王欧利庞：**是你…遭人唾弃的野兽，你真的拒绝了死亡。预言不假…朕的悬锋王朝，终究要结束在你手里……

**Mydei:** Defy? You're wrong. Death fears me. It's the reason I have this body of steel.

> **万敌：**拒绝？你错了，是死亡惧怕我。拜它所赐，我才有了这副钢铁之躯。

**Mydei:** As for you, you already stand at death's door, father. You were the one who threw me into the Sea of Souls, the one who tried in vain to enslave a Titan, and the one who desecrated the body of a god...

> **万敌：**而你，你已病入膏肓了，父亲。将我抛入冥海的人是你，妄图奴役泰坦、亵渎神体的人也是你……

**Mydei:** HKS! You're a weak king! So weak that even the lowliest hyena is a more noble existence than you!

> **万敌：** HKS！软弱的王，哪怕是最卑劣的鬣狗，也比你高贵千倍！

**Mydei:** And now, fate's reckoning is upon you!

> **万敌：**而现在，命运的复仇找上你了！

**Old King Eurypon:** Then swing your blade! Take my crown, coronate yourself with an old king's blood, and inherit it all... Become the new rightful king of Kremnos.

> **先王欧利庞：**那就挥剑吧！拿走我的王冠，用先王的血加冕，继承一切…名正言顺，成为悬锋的新王。

**Mydei:** No, I don't want the crown. Listen — If there's only one end for the kings of Kremnos, then the crown means nothing to me...

> **万敌：**不，我不要王冠。听好了——倘若悬锋的王只有一种宿命，那它对我毫无意义……

**Mydei:** All I want is your life! To avenge mother and my comrades!

> **万敌：**我只要你的命！为了给母亲和同袍复仇，仅此而已！

**Old King Eurypon:** But the walls of Kremnos will not fall with me. Do you truly think you can escape fate's grasp, my son?

> **先王欧利庞：**但悬锋的城墙不会随我一同崩塌…吾儿，你以为自己逃得出命运的掌心？

**Old King Eurypon:** Hahaha... You and I, we're both the same... We feast on strife and fear...

> **先王欧利庞：**哈哈哈…都一样，你和我…我们都把纷争和恐惧当做食粮……

**Old King Eurypon:** ...Now, kill me!

> **先王欧利庞：**…现在，杀了我！

**Old King Eurypon:** The son of Gorgo is destined to be crowned in blood!

> **先王欧利庞：**歌耳戈之子，注定要浴血代冠——！

**Mydei:** ...

> **万敌：**……

**Mydei:** ...

> **万敌：**…哎。

**Mydei:** Krateros...

> **万敌：**克拉特鲁斯……

**Mydei:** He said the Grand Craftsman Chartonus might have some suggestions. Might as well go see him.

> **万敌：**那家伙说，也许能从大工匠那得到建议…哈托努斯，去见他吧。

*(Dialogue continues into the next step)*

### Seek advice from Grand Craftsman Chartonus

*(Dialogue continues from the previous step)*

**Mydei:** Grand Craftsman, how have you been?

> **万敌：**大工匠，近来可好？

**Chartonus:** King of Kremnos, it is. That you would come, I knew.

> **哈托努斯：**悬锋的王，是你。你会来，我有预感。

**Mydei:** You knew I was coming?

> **万敌：**你知道我会来？

**Chartonus:** Your matters, I've heard. The path forward, you seek. Many questions, you have.

> **哈托努斯：**我听说了，你的事。前进的方向，你在寻找；疑问，有许多。

**Chartonus:** But before that... Take this.

> **哈托努斯：**但在那之前——这个，收下。

**Mydei:** What's this...?

> **万敌：**这是…？

**Chartonus:** A signet ring. For the king of Kremnos. Belonged to your mother. Recast and restore it... I did.

> **哈托努斯：**悬锋，王者的印戒，属于你母亲。重铸、修复…由我。

**Mydei:** What... Mother threw it into the Sea of Souls...

> **万敌：**母亲明明将它抛入冥海了……

**Chartonus:** A long time to fish out, it took. Very fortunate, we were.

> **哈托努斯：**打捞，花了很久。运气很好，我们。

**Mydei:** I can hardly imagine how much patience and courage it must have taken to find such a small signet ring in the Sea of Souls. I owe you one.

> **万敌：**要从冥海中捞出一枚小小的印戒，我无法想象需要多少耐心和勇气。我欠你一个人情。

**Chartonus:** Gratitude, there is no need for. Repayment for saving me, this is.

> **哈托努斯：**感谢，不必。你的救命之恩，我要报答。

**Chartonus:** Questions, you bring with you. Ask, now.

> **哈托努斯：**疑问，你带着它来。说吧，现在。

**Mydei:** ...

> **万敌：**……

**Mydei:** The fate I've always denied has finally caught up to me.

> **万敌：**我一直否定的命运，终于还是找上了门。

**Mydei:** By all rights, I should accept Strife's authority. It would make everyone happy: the Chrysos Heirs, the Council of Elders, the Kremnoans, even the bards who will write it all into song...

> **万敌：**我理应接过「纷争」的权柄。如此一来，所有人都会满意：黄金裔、元老院、悬锋族人，还有谱写诗歌的吟游诗人……

**Chartonus:** Yet hesitate still, you do. Hesitate, why do you, young king?

> **哈托努斯：**但还在犹豫，你。年轻的王，为何踌躇？

**Mydei:** ...Because of my people.

> **万敌：**…因为我的族人。

**Mydei:** Be they men, women, the elderly, or children... the hearts of all Kremnoans yearn for home. They've all been waiting for this day, for the day their king returns.

> **万敌：**男人、女人、长者、孩童…悬锋人对归乡的渴望深入心髓，他们一直在等待这天，企盼王者的归来。

**Mydei:** The children of Strife would then be able to go home and embrace the old traditions — never mind that most of them have only ever been heard in these so-called "traditions" in song. They would then gather around their king and throw themselves into battle.

> **万敌：**如此，纷争的子民便能够还乡，拥抱传统——即便他们中的大多数人只在史诗中听闻过那「传统」——然后，他们便能围绕在王的身旁，投身战场。

**Chartonus:** This is not a good thing, you believe.

> **哈托努斯：**这不是一件好事，你认为。

**Mydei:** ...The Kremnoans have been trapped in a cycle of bloodshed for the past one thousand years: They seek vengeance, start wars, become prosperous, then decline... How much innocent blood flowed into the River of Souls in all of those years? How many needless sacrifices could we have avoided?

> **万敌：**…过去的千年，悬锋人活在血腥的轮回里。复仇、征战、昌盛、衰败…那段历史中，多少无辜者的血汇入冥河，多少无谓的牺牲可以被避免？

**Mydei:** If I accept that fate and become the totem of Strife, then lead my people into repeating the same mistakes... then of course I would be terrified. I'd be terrified of becoming king.

> **万敌：**若要我接受这命运，成为「纷争」的图腾，并带领我的族人重蹈覆辙…那我的确会感到恐惧，对「成王」的恐惧。

**Chartonus:** ...

> **哈托努斯：**……

**Chartonus:** A different kind of Kremnoan, you truly are...

> **哈托努斯：**不同的悬锋人，你果然是……

Switching to Tribbie's POV...

In order to reconcile the Kremnoans and the Okhemans, Tribbie, Trianne, and Trinnon decided to visit the Titan and retrieve their memories...

## 110　Passages, Knocking Echoes in Dreams　门扉啊，叩声仍激荡梦中

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域："Eternal Holy City" Okhema　|　角色：Tribios; Mortis; Tribbie; Aglaea; Phainon; Anaxa; Trianne; Trinnon; Mydei; Castorice; Dan Heng; Flame Reaver; Trailblazer; Hyacine; Okhema Scout　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Memories, Veiled in Blazing Mist'' completed

### Fall asleep and enter Tribbie's dream

> *A dream carries us back to memories long faded by time, and this dream mirrors the profound fears within our hearts.*

*(Enter Exploratory Excursion Depths of Tribbie's Dream)*

...

Moments later, in Tribbie's room...

**???:** Momma, Momma! I had a dream last night.

> **？？？：**妈妈，妈妈！我昨晚做了一个梦。

**Gentle Mother:** Oh? Now, what did you dream about, my darling Tribios?

> **温柔的母亲：**哦？梦到什么了，缇里西庇俄丝，我的好女孩？

**Tribios:** I had a dream that I turned into many mes! Then we made the moon into a ship and made the stars into our sails! Then we floated on the ocean and let the wind blow us in any direction it wanted!

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**我梦见自己变成了好多好多个！我们一起把月亮当做船，把星星当做帆，在海上漂来漂去。风吹向哪里，我们就漂到哪里！

**Gentle Mother:** Well that sounds like a lovely dream.

> **温柔的母亲：**听起来是个美梦呢。

**Tribios:** Yeah! The ocean and the sky were really, really dark, but there were so many of me that I wasn't scared at all. Because we'd all sing together!

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**是啊！虽然大海，还有天空，都好黑好黑。但是好多好多个我一点都不害怕，因为我们会一起唱歌！

**Tribios:** The ocean wind blew us onto an island where the wind tasted like flowers. There were bleating sheep and chirping birds inviting us to be their guests. There were so many animals on the island, but none of them ever fought!

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**海风把我们送往一座小岛，那里的风有花朵的味道。还有绵羊咩咩，小鸟啾啾，欢迎我们去作客。岛上有好多动物，但大家从来不争吵！

**Gentle Mother:** Janus' Holy Maidens can receive guidance from the god of passages. Perhaps what you saw in your dream is a divine land somewhere in Amphoreus.

> **温柔的母亲：**雅努斯的圣女能聆听门神的指引，也许你梦见的，就是翁法罗斯某处仙境的景象哦。

**Tribios:** Y—You think so?

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**…这、这样吗？

**Gentle Mother:** What's wrong, Tribios? You look unhappy.

> **温柔的母亲：**怎么了，缇里西庇俄丝，你好像不开心？

**Tribios:** Because... on that same island, I saw a dark mountain on the other side of the ocean. And it was very, very tall.

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**因为…在那座小岛上，我还看见海的另一边有片黑色的山，很高很高。

**Tribios:** The mountain looked alive, and it kept getting closer to the island. Then I realized that it wasn't a mountain, but a very, very tall wave made of water... When the wave smashed against the island, it looked like it was going to eat the island up. The small animals tried to chase it away, but they couldn't stop it...

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**那山就像活着似的，一直在向小岛靠近。后来我才发现，那根本不是山，是很高很高的海浪…海浪拍在小岛上，像是要把它吃掉。小动物们想把它赶走，但根本拦不住……

**Tribios:** Then there was a "bang!" and I woke up.

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**最后「轰——！」的一下，我就醒了。

**Gentle Mother:** Hm... A dark wave...

> **温柔的母亲：**嗯…黑色的海浪啊……

**Tribios:** Momma, does something that scary exist in real life?

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**妈妈，现实里有这么可怕的东西吗？

**Gentle Mother:** Yes... Unfortunately, it does exist in Amphoreus, but it's very far away from our temple.

> **温柔的母亲：**嗯…很遗憾，它确实存在于翁法罗斯，只是离我们的圣殿还很遥远。

**Gentle Mother:** We call it the "black tide," though it has nothing to do with water. It has no shape or form, but it can swallow up animals, humans, and even Titans... turning them all into monsters.

> **温柔的母亲：**人们称之为「黑潮」，但其实和大海无关。它无形无貌，却能把动物、人、甚至泰坦一口吞下…把他们变成黑色的怪物。

**Tribios:** Ooh... The black tide is a baddie! I don't want everyone to turn into its food. Can't we chase it away?

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**喔…黑潮好坏！我不想让大家变成它的小面包，有办法赶走这个家伙吗？

**Gentle Mother:** Of course, my dear. You only need to behave well and listen to what the adults tell you, study all the knowledge and etiquette you need, then grow up and become a proper Holy Maiden...

> **温柔的母亲：**当然了，好孩子。只是你要乖乖的，听大人的话，好好学习知识和礼仪，成长为合格的圣女……

**Gentle Mother:** The divine Janus will then give you guidance and help you light the way for the people.

> **温柔的母亲：**神圣的雅努斯就会降下指引，让你为人们点明正确的道路。

**Tribios:** Wow! I'm gonna eat a lot and study a lot and become a Holy Maiden!

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**呼哇…好好吃饭，好好学习，成为圣女！

**Tribios:** But, Momma, you said the black tide can swallow up Titans too. What do we do if even Janus is eaten?

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**可是，妈妈刚才说，黑潮也会吃掉泰坦…如果连雅努斯也被吃掉了，那该怎么办呢？

**Gentle Mother:** If that happens... then the Holy Maiden will have to be the one to guide people on the right path. After all, we are the human avatars of the god of passages, yeah?

> **温柔的母亲：**那…就得由圣女来为人们指点迷津了。毕竟我们就是门神在人间的化身，对不对？

**Tribios:** Yeah!

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**对！

**Gentle Mother:** Hehe. But one person alone is not enough. Once you're older, you'll make a lot of friends. You must do your best to unite them because that's also our duty as priests.

> **温柔的母亲：**呵呵。不过，只有一个人是不够的。等你长大了，一定会遇见许多朋友。你要尽全力团结他们，这也是我们身为祭司的职责。

**Tribios:** Friends? Are they also like me and Momma, with pretty red hair?

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**朋友？他们都像妈妈和我一样，有好看的红头发吗？

**Gentle Mother:** They might not, but they'll certainly be just as smart and kind as you.

> **温柔的母亲：**这个不一定。但他们肯定和你一样聪明，一样善良。

**Tribbie:** (Mama...)

> **缇宝：**（妈妈……）

**Tribbie:** (Am I... dreaming?)

> **缇宝：**（我…在做梦吗？）

**Gentle Mother:** Don't be silly, dear. Of course you're not dreaming. I've always been by your side.

> **温柔的母亲：**傻孩子，当然不是梦了。妈妈一直都在你的身边。

**Tribbie:** (What...)

> **缇宝：**（什么……）

**Gentle Mother:** Now be a good girl and call your friends over too...

> **温柔的母亲：**我的好女孩，别闹别扭了。我们把你的伙伴也一起叫来——

**Gentle Mother:** Let's play hide-and-seek just like we used to, okay? My Tribios is the smartest girl. I'm sure you remember where everyone is hiding.

> **温柔的母亲：**就像平日那样，来玩「捉迷藏」，好不好？缇里西庇俄丝的脑瓜最聪明了，你肯定记得大家都躲在哪里。

**Tribbie:** (The voice... sounds so far away.)

> **缇宝：**（声音…好遥远。）

**Tribbie:** I don't... remember... Who am I looking for?

> **缇宝：**我…不记得了…我要找谁？

**Gentle Mother:** Your friends who will chase the black tide away alongside you, of course.

> **温柔的母亲：**当然是要和你一起，赶走黑潮的同伴啦。

### Find missing memories (0/3)

**Tribbie:** (Is this... from my childhood? I don't remember...)

*(Investigate blackboard, optional)*

**Tribbie:** (A blackboard? And what is that drawing...?)

**Tribbie:** (It doesn't ring a bell at all. Perhaps it's just a childhood doodle.)

*(Investigate bookshelves, optional)*

**Tribbie:** (It's packed full... Mama must have left all of these for me.)

**Tribbie:** (All the words are blurred out... Could it be that my memory is fading?)

*(Investigate lounge chair, optional)*

**Tribbie:** (I used to sit here and listen to Mama tell stories...)

**Tribbie:** (Oh, how I wish to see you again... Mama.)

> **缇宝：**（这是我…童年的事吗？想不起来了……）

*(Approach the doll on two chests)*

**???:** Use the spindle to connect everyone!

> **？？？：**用纺锤，连系大家！

*(Interact with the doll)*

**Gentle Mother:** There will be an amazing captain on that big ship. She's cheerful, optimistic, bold, and cautious. She'll be able to get everyone together.

> **温柔的母亲：**在那艘好大的船上，会有一位厉害的船长。她开朗乐观，胆大心细，能把大家联系在一起。

**Tribios:** Wow, that's amazing! Can I be the captain?

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**呼哇，好厉害！我可以当这个船长吗？

**Gentle Mother:** Of course you can! But you'll find someone else who's better for the job, my dear.

> **温柔的母亲：**当然可以。但我的好女孩，你会找到更合适的人选。

**Gentle Mother:** I think that person lives in a beautiful temple that sits atop a hillside covered in golden fig trees.

> **温柔的母亲：**我想，那人应该住在一座华丽的神殿里，那神殿矗立在长满金色无花果树的山坡上。

**Gentle Mother:** There's a noble girl there who's very smart but also very delicate.

> **温柔的母亲：**那里有一位尊贵的女孩，她聪慧过人，但也细腻敏感。

**Tribios:** Just like Momma...?

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**就像…妈妈一样？

**Gentle Mother:** Perhaps. But before we find the captain, the ship also needs someone who can navigate it. Who could that be?

> **温柔的母亲：**或许吧。不过在找到船长前，小船还需要一位领航的伙伴，她是谁呢？

**Tribios:** Mmm... Let me think...

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**呜，我想想……

**Tribios:** I know! She's... "Tribbie"!

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**我知道了！她就是——「缇宝」！

**Tribbie:** (I named this doll... after myself?)

> **缇宝：**（我给这个娃娃…取了和自己一样的名字？）

**Tribbie:** (I don't remember at all... Is this a dream, or something that happened in the past?)

> **缇宝：**（完全不记得了…这是梦，还是发生过的事？）

*(Approach the chest)*

**???:** Bad chest, you can't lock me away!

> **？？？：**坏箱子，关不住我！

*(Interact with the chest)*

**Tribbie:** (Huh? Why is this doll inside a chest...?)

> **缇宝：**（咦？这只娃娃，为什么在箱子里……）

**Tribbie:** (It's holding a... sword? Why...)

> **缇宝：**（它手里的是…剑？为什么……）

**Gentle Mother:** Many years later, many brave warriors will board this ship. They are skilled combatants who fear no danger.

> **温柔的母亲：**很久以后，会有许多勇敢的战士登上这艘船。他们武艺高强，不畏艰险。

**Tribios:** Ooh... I can learn how to fight too and protect everybody!

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**唔…我也可以学着战斗，保护大家！

**Gentle Mother:** Silly girl, you're Janusopolis' Holy Maiden. It's not your duty to fight. There will be many others to protect you.

> **温柔的母亲：**傻孩子，你是雅努萨波利斯的圣女。战斗不是你的职责，会有许多人来保护你的。

**Tribios:** Alright, then where will they come from?

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**好吧，那他们都来自哪里呢？

**Gentle Mother:** I believe one warrior hails from a lovely village where bulrushes flourish, while another comes from a magnificent royal city. Though each has their own troubles, both are ready to lend their strength for the ship's long voyage.

> **温柔的母亲：**我想，其中一位战士来自蔺草遍野的美丽村庄，还有一位来自宏伟的王城。他们都有各自的烦恼，但也愿意为小船的远航献出力量。

**Tribios:** In that case, let "Trianne" fight by their side!

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**既然这样，就让「缇安」和他们并肩作战吧！

**Tribios:** This way, they'll always stay on course!

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**这样，大家就不会迷茫了！

**Tribbie:** (How could I have known the name "Trianne" back then?)

> **缇宝：**（我当时…怎么会知道「缇安」这个名字？）

**Tribbie:** (Was it just a coincidence, or...)

> **缇宝：**（是巧合，还是……）

*(Approach the doll on the table with the scrolls)*

**???:** Learning can change your destiny!

> **？？？：**学习，改变命运！

*(Interact with the doll)*

**Gentle Mother:** When navigating at sea, you need to know both astronomy and geography.

> **温柔的母亲：**在海上航行，一定要上知天文，下知地理。

**Tribios:** Ugh... isn't Janus' knowledge enough?

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**呜…只学习雅努斯的知识还不够吗？

**Gentle Mother:** Oh, sweetie. The ocean isn't like the land. It's easy to get lost. That's why it's important to learn to recognize the miracle of Aquila.

> **温柔的母亲：**当然啦。大海不是陆地，一不小心就会迷失方向。所以，学会辨认艾格勒的神迹可是很重要的。

**Tribios:** Alright! Even though my brain is already super-duper smart, I'll still try my very best!

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**好吧！虽然我的脑瓜已经特别特别聪明了，但我还是会努力！

**Gentle Mother:** Cerces also favors children who try their best. They will send a wise scholar as everyone's teacher to help you reach your dreams.

> **温柔的母亲：**瑟希斯也会眷顾认真的孩子。它会派出知识渊博的学者，当大家的老师，帮助你实现理想。

> **温柔的母亲：**为什么不呢？不过，如果想要教书育人，这么好动可不行。

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**那…就让「缇宁」来！她很乖，肯定能做个好老师。

> **缇宝：**（我...在做梦吗？）

**Gentle Mother:** "Happiness abounded in this lush land by gods chosen. Giants raised their glasses in a toast beheld by the twelve constellations."

> **温柔的母亲：**「神明眷顾沃土，遍地欣欣如火。十二星宿如目，巨人举杯对酌。」

**Gentle Mother:** "Three carved the heavens and the earth, three wove the threads of fate. Three molded life with their hands, and three guided calamity's gate."

> **温柔的母亲：**「三者开辟天地，三者编织命运。三者捏塑生命，三者引渡灾祸。」

**Gentle Mother:** "They declared the world too unstirring, hence wished endless laughter upon the living."

> **温柔的母亲：**「它们说：世界太过沉寂。只愿见，生灵欢笑不息。」

> **温柔的母亲：**「于是便有了我、你，编织言语和歌谣，诞下爱情与知己。」

> **温柔的母亲：**「自此，创生已毕。谁来背负灵魂的重，换取世人步伐之轻？」

> **温柔的母亲：**「伟岸的刻法勒，全知的父，它身躯伟岸，却甘将眼睑垂低。」

> **温柔的母亲：**「黎明的光沉负于肩，金色的血落向大地。」

> **温柔的母亲：**「汇作一条滚烫的河，流遍世间英雄末裔。」

### Speak with Aglaea

**Tribbie:** Mama...!

> **缇宝：**妈妈…！

**Tribbie:** ...

> **缇宝：**……

**Tribbie:** ...Are we still in a dream?

> **缇宝：**…*我们*还在梦里吗？

**Aglaea:** Did you have a nightmare?

> **阿格莱雅：**做噩梦了么？

**Tribbie:** ...Maybe. But it's been a long time since we had any dreams.

> **缇宝：**…或许吧。明明*我们*已经好久没做过梦了。

**Aglaea:** It's not yet the Entry Hour. You still have plenty of time to rest.

> **阿格莱雅：**现在还未到门扉时，你还可以休息很久。

**Tribbie:** No... Our heart is still racing.

> **缇宝：**不了…*我们*的心脏还在怦怦跳呢。

**Aglaea:** It must be that reckless Kremnoan soldier who startled Trinnon — and you as well. If you want, I can pierce through his chest with the golden thread right now.

> **阿格莱雅：**许是那粗鲁的悬锋老兵让缇宁受了惊吓，也连带影响了你。若你有意，我现在就可以用金线荡破他的胸膛。

**Tribbie:** Agy...! Cut it out with the scary talk...

> **缇宝：**阿雅…！别总说这种吓人的话……

**Aglaea:** I'm just kidding. If I were serious, I wouldn't have waited until now.

> **阿格莱雅：**这是玩笑话。若有必要，我不会等到现在。

**Tribbie:** Ah...

> **缇宝：**哎……

**Aglaea:** Do you want to talk about the dream? Keeping it bottled up is not good for your health.

> **阿格莱雅：**想聊聊那场梦么？若长久郁结心中，容易落成病根。

**Tribbie:** We... had a dream about Mama.

> **缇宝：** *我们*…梦到妈妈了。

**Tribbie:** The dream itself was nothing... It's just the timing that feels a bit unsettling.

> **缇宝：**不过，梦本身没什么…只是无缘无故在这时做梦，令人有些不安。

**Aglaea:** You mean...

> **阿格莱雅：**你指的是……

**Tribbie:** ...It's Trianne.

> **缇宝：**…就是缇安。

**Tribbie:** Even if we refrain from ever using the power of the Century Gate again... her soul continues to slowly drift away from her body.

> **缇宝：**就算*我们*从今往后，再也不使用「百界门」的力量…她的魂息也在慢慢飘离身体。

**Tribbie:** There isn't much time left.

> **缇宝：**时间…恐怕已经所剩无几了。

**Aglaea:** Perhaps... it wouldn't be such a bad idea to let her relinquish her role as the "gatekeeper" and live out her days as a carefree child.

> **阿格莱雅：**或许…让她提前让渡「门匠」的职责，就作为一个普通的孩子无忧无虑地活到最后，也不失为一件好事。

**Tribbie:** You have a point, Agy. But... our decision has already been made on that matter.

> **缇宝：**我理解你的想法，阿雅。但在这件事上…*我们*已经下定决心。

**Aglaea:** I believe there's still another way.

> **阿格莱雅：**我想，此事还有回旋的余地。

**Tribbie:** Souls will be spent until they dissolve into dust — such is our fate.

> **缇宝：**灵魂磨损，直至归于尘土，那就是*我们*的命运。

**Tribbie:** Once, we had a whole and connected body. If that body were a tree, then its trunk and branches would share the same fate, decaying at the same pace.

> **缇宝：**曾经，*我们*也有完整相连的身体。如果把那副躯体比作大树，那么树干和枝叶都会因命运使然，以相同的速度凋零。

**Tribbie:** Now, we are fragmented into numerous selves... Despite the absence of a trunk, we are offered another possibility...

> **缇宝：**现在，*我们*分成了许许多多个自己…虽然失去了树干，却也多了一种选择……

**Tribbie:** ...We can choose how our branches will perish in a way that is most beneficial to the Flame-Chase Journey.

> **缇宝：**…*我们*能以对逐火之旅最有益的方式，决定枝叶以何种形式腐朽。

**Aglaea:** Compared with saying farewell to one person... watching many fade away before me is even more sorrowful.

> **阿格莱雅：**比起与一人道别…看着许多人在眼前陆续消逝更令人伤怀。

**Tribbie:** But "advancing" is the destined end of Janus' trial. There's no retreat in our path.

> **缇宝：**可…毕竟「前进」是雅努斯试炼注定的结果，*我们*从来没有退路可言。

**Tribbie:** Don't feel sad about saying goodbye to us. We have already agreed on this, right?

> **缇宝：**不要为和*我们*道别伤怀——我们早就约定好了，对不对？

**Aglaea:** It won't be long before I forget what sorrow tastes like.

> **阿格莱雅：**用不了多久，我可能就品尝不出悲伤的滋味了。

**Tribbie:** ...

> **缇宝：**……

**Tribbie:** So, what would you like for breakfast, Agy?

> **缇宝：**阿雅，你早餐想吃什么？

**Aglaea:** What? Where did that come from...?

> **阿格莱雅：**什么？怎么突然……

**Tribbie:** After all this talking, we are feeling a bit hungry. Plus, it's nearly the Entry Hour now.

> **缇宝：**聊了这么久，肚子有点饿啦。况且，不是快到门扉时了嘛。

**Tribbie:** Do you remember when we first met, Agy? You were still a little one, and we caught you secretly eating oatmeal late at night...

> **缇宝：**说到这个，阿雅，你还记得吗？应该是刚见面不久的时候，你也还是个小不点，半夜嘴馋偷吃燕麦粥被*我们*逮住……

**Aglaea:** Heh, while my emotions might be fading, those memories are still as vivid as ever, teacher.

> **阿格莱雅：**呵…我的情感在流逝，但那些片段——我全都记得，吾师。

**Aglaea:** Please, let me do it. What would you like to eat?

> **阿格莱雅：**还是我去吧。你想吃些什么？

**Tribbie:** It's fine. Leave it to us. You should rest for now. It won't take long, and we will call you once it's done.

> **缇宝：**不用不用，交给*我们*。你先休息会儿，很快的，*我们*准备好了就来叫你。

**Aglaea:** ...In that case, thank you.

> **阿格莱雅：**…好，那就麻烦你了。

**Tribbie:** Then... See you tomorrow, Agy!

> **缇宝：**那…明早见，阿雅！

**Tribbie:** (Nevertheless, that nightmare is ominous. Memories are slipping away... even the image of Mama...)

> **缇宝：**（话是这么说，但噩梦是个坏兆头。记忆越来越模糊不清了，连妈妈的样子都……）

**Tribbie:** (Just as expected, another trip must be made to the Abyss of Fate. Hopefully, Oronyx will be willing to help us.)

> **缇宝：**（果然…还是得先去趟命运重渊才行。希望欧洛尼斯愿意帮帮*我们*。）

### Observe Phainon conversing with Anaxa

> *Phainon finds the confined Anaxa, apparently not just for catching up.*

At the same time, somewhere in Okhema

**Anaxa:** ...

> **那刻夏：**……

**Phainon:** Long time no see, Professor Anaxa.

> **白厄：**那刻夏老师，好久不见。

**Anaxa:** Who are you?

> **那刻夏：**你是谁？

**Phainon:** It's been a while. I'm the one who once overthrew the entire classroom in that spiritual physics lecture.

> **白厄：**久违了，我是那个在灵魂物理学的课堂上掀翻了教室的学生。

**Anaxa:** Oh, it's you.

> **那刻夏：**哦，是你啊。

**Phainon:** No way... Do you really still remember that, professor?

> **白厄：**不是吧，老师，我就随口一说…你真记得这事？

**Anaxa:** I remember that glib tongue of yours. What brings you here? Surely you're not looking to reminisce.

> **那刻夏：**还记得你的油嘴滑舌。找我做什么，可不只是为了叙旧吧？

**Phainon:** You could tell?

> **白厄：**这你都看出来了？

**Anaxa:** Of course. You only beat around the bush when you have a hidden agenda.

> **那刻夏：**当然，你只有在别有企图时，才会这么拐弯抹角。

**Phainon:** That's not true. I'm here to seek enlightenment, as always...

> **白厄：**哪里的话。我可是来诚心求教的，一如既往……

**Phainon:** Could you please teach me everything you know about the black-robed swordmaster?

> **白厄：**还请不吝赐教：有关您所知的，那位黑袍剑士的一切。

**Anaxa:** Hmph. Rumors sure travel fast.

> **那刻夏：**哼，流言传得真快啊。

**Anaxa:** Unfortunately I, too, know nothing about it. I can only tell you that it is clad in a black robe and wields a greatsword... At least, it looks like a greatsword. It's shaped like a twisted half-sun...

> **那刻夏：**可惜，我对它也一无所知，能告诉你的：只有它身披黑袍，手持重剑…大概是重剑吧，形如半轮扭曲的太阳……

**Anaxa:** And a peculiar dagger resembling a crescent moon.

> **那刻夏：**还有一柄古怪的匕首，如同一弯新月。

**Phainon:** ...

> **白厄：**……

**Anaxa:** By the way, take this with you—

> **那刻夏：**对了，这个拿去——

**Anaxa:** I got it from the sneak attack. It's yours now.

> **那刻夏：**偷袭时得手的战利品，送你了。

**Phainon:** ...

> **白厄：**……

**Phainon:** I was right... It is this thing. The one who torched Aedes Elysiae to the ground... and killed everyone.

> **白厄：**不会错…就是它。烧毁了哀丽秘榭…杀害所有人的凶手。

**Anaxa:** Don't try to be a hero. No one in Okhema stands a chance against that thing right now.

> **那刻夏：**奉劝你别逞英雄。眼下的奥赫玛，恐怕没人是它的对手。

**Phainon:** Is that what the Titan said as well?

> **白厄：**那位泰坦也是这么说的？

**Anaxa:** Hmph, not at first. But the god relented after having had a taste of its power. Even with a god possessing me, I couldn't fight against that blade...

> **那刻夏：**哼，开始不是，但领教到它的厉害后也甘拜下风了。就算请神上身，我也难以招架对方的锋刃……

**Anaxa:** That incredible power doesn't seem to be granted by any Titan.

> **那刻夏：**那不可思议的力量，不像是任何一位泰坦的赐福。

**Phainon:** Are you suggesting that... it originates from beyond Amphoreus?

> **白厄：**你想主张…它来自翁法罗斯之外？

**Anaxa:** It's possible. Much like the black tide, no?

> **那刻夏：**不无可能。就跟黑潮一样，不是么？

**Phainon:** ...Nonetheless, it's an enemy we must overcome.

> **白厄：**…无论如何，那是我们必须战胜的敌人。

**Anaxa:** We? Drop the pipe dream, Phainon. No one wants to see you fall by their blade.

> **那刻夏：**我们？别异想天开了，白厄，没人想看你横死在它的剑下。

**Phainon:** His mere presence will endanger the holy city and the Flame-Chase.

> **白厄：**只要那家伙存在，就会威胁到圣城，还有逐火的征程。

**Anaxa:** You know that I don't believe—

*(Quick Read)*

Saffron Secret Recipes

**To Mr. Wincolm:** ... For many years, Janusopolis has relied on the rose myrtle to obtain the boon of the gods. We have spent five or six years now digging out its roots and cutting back its branches. Now, this red tree is on the verge of collapse and has long since ceased to serve its purpose, yet it still seeks to spread out its canopy and eclipse Janusopolis from the glorious rays. We have a secret plan to fell this tree. We just need to wait for the ceremony to make a small move, and the rose myrtle will slip and fall into the abyss... Janusopolis is built on a mountainside — there are more than a few cases of people who have lost their footing in its history. ... As for the next sacred tree, we were planning on selecting another puppet. As for the rose myrtle's daughter, Tribios... We'll call her saffron for now. In my eyes, I think it would be fine to just find an excuse to off saffron. However, Codex the acolyte is extremely insistent on raising her to become the next sacred tree. She has undergone related education since she was a young girl, which would save a lot of work on our part. As for her identity as the daughter of rose myrtle... She is still young and has not yet grown wings. If we can just keep her grounded, the power of the divine oracle will remain tightly in our grasp. We both know that the saffron's most outstanding property is that it can be cooked in so many ways: Put in porridge, stewed, or fried... No matter how you cook it, this plant will always be a delicacy. ... Dannalio

**Phainon:** ...I understand. "Hatred is a hero's deadliest poison." I have no intention of fighting alone. If that swordmaster really is related to the black tide, the Chrysos Heirs must not take them lightly.

> **白厄：**…当然。「仇恨是杀死英雄的毒酒」——我无意孤军奋战。若那剑士真与黑潮有关，绝不是黄金裔能轻视的对手。

**Phainon:** I have indeed planned to return to the holy city and discuss the strategy with Aglaea. We'll need all the help we can get for this encirclement.

> **白厄：**我本就打算返回圣城，与阿格莱雅共议战略。这是一场围剿，我们需要更多人的帮助。

**Dan Heng:** Why don't we split up as previously discussed? Hyacine and I will stay to regroup with the reinforcements. You go back to the holy city with everyone else and tell Aglaea about your plan.

> **丹恒：**不如按照先前分工，我和风堇留下来接应援兵。你带剩下的人返回圣城，告诉阿格莱雅你们的计划吧。

**Trinnon:** Trinnon will stay in the Abyss, too. That way, both sides can communicate with each other if anything happens.

Returning to the Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

**Aglaea:** Tell me, what makes you think this won't end with us losing another Coreflame?

**Aglaea:** It's very clever, using the element of surprise instead of brute strength.

**Mem:** Well, let's just say that (Trailblazer) and I can do anything as long as we're together!

> **阿格莱雅：**几位有所不知：吾师失踪的同时，元老院也已告急。凯妮斯一派还是得知了树庭的事，暗中开始造势，恐怕在下一次公民大会前就会有所行动。

> **阿格莱雅：**目光短浅的野心家，大概是觉得黄金裔的利用价值将尽，准备提前布局吧？总有一天，我们也必须面对「人」的问题。

> **阿格莱雅：**白厄，我希望与你达成共识：我们要将那盗火行者一举拿下，斩获战果。此后，面对元老院的挑战，我们也将有自己的底牌。

**Phainon:** Of course. You may count on me to lead the charge.

> **阿格莱雅：**抱歉，开拓者阁下，如今奥赫玛内忧外患，又将你推上浪尖。

> **缇宝：**虽然对「百界门」的掌握不如缇安，但只要各位能争取些时间，*我*也可以开启一扇漂亮的门。

**Aglaea:** As for whether we can take back the Coreflame... that will be up to Anaxa's capabilities.

Vortex of Genesis, Okhema

**Hephaestion:** Farewell, my dear friend. You must... lead us back home...

**Krateros:** If you're truly capable of leading our people beyond this bloodstained fate, then prove it to me once more, Mydeimos.

> **克拉特鲁斯：**你若真能带领族人打破这染血的命运，就再次证明给我看吧，迈德漠斯。

**Mydei:** ...

> **万敌：**……

**Phainon:** I knew you'd be here.

> **白厄：**你果然在这里。

**Phainon:** That's right. It's going to be a tough battle, and our destination is... turns out to be Castrum Kremnos.

> **白厄：**没错，这会是无比艰难的一仗。目的地…还是悬锋城。

**Mydei:** Is that why you're here? To pray to the dead Nikador?

> **万敌：**所以，你是来向死去的尼卡多利祈祷的？

**Phainon:** No, just the opposite. I'm here to tell you...

**Mydei:** Hmph...

> **万敌：**哼……

**Mydei:** Have you heard the story of Geocles the Mountainbreaker?

> **万敌：**「开山者」吉奥刻勒斯——他的故事，你听过么？

**Phainon:** Sounds like you've spoken with Chartonus. That's the name of the legendary Mountain Dweller, right?

> **白厄：**看来你和哈托努斯聊过了啊。这个名字，是那位传说中的山之民吧？

**Mydei:** Let's have a chat before we go our separate ways.

> **万敌：**分别前，再多说几句吧。

**Mydei:** Chartonus told me that back in ancient times, the Mountain Dwellers lived in seclusion. They spent their days quarrying and gathering, living a simple and peaceful life away from strife.

> **万敌：**哈托努斯告诉我，远古，山之民们都隐居在山丘中。他们终日挖掘、采集…过着平和质朴的生活，远离纷争。

**Mydei:** But Geocles was different. He resisted their traditional way of life, yearning for the world beyond the mountain. Their safe but humdrum culture was like fetters around him.

> **万敌：**但吉奥刻勒斯是个异类，他不认可传统，向往山外的世界。温吞的习俗，在他眼中仿佛一座监牢。

**Phainon:** He sounds a lot like you.

> **白厄：**和你的处境很像啊。

**Mydei:** Yes, that is why he, too, faced the same dilemma as I do. One day, during one of Geocles' many ventures beyond the mountain, he spied a battalion of Ladonians stationed at the foot of the mountain.

> **万敌：**没错，所以他也必须面对同样的抉择：某日，吉奥刻勒斯一如往常，翻出山丘。但他看见，山脚下驻扎了一整支拉冬人的营队。

**Mydei:** The Ladonians were at war with their archenemies, the Campanians, and they used the mountain as a base to carry out guerrilla warfare operations. When Geocles, who had taught himself the common tongue, approached a soldier, he learned a shocking piece of news:

> **万敌：**那群拉冬人正和宿敌坎帕人交战，把山岳当成了游击的本营。吉奥刻勒斯自学了通用语，他从士兵口中套出了情报——

**Mydei:** The Ladonians were planning to plant mines and explosives all over the mountain, and then lure their enemies within. With danger lurking with every step, the Mountain Dwellers' homes were no longer safe. Geocles quickly returned to his village to bring the news to his people, but...

> **万敌：**——拉冬人计划在山间布满暗雷和炸药，并诱敌深入。那代表山之民的家园将不再安全，从此会危机四伏。吉奥刻勒斯即刻返回村落，将消息告知族人。然而……

**Phainon:** Let me guess: They refused change and chose to put up with the situation instead?

> **白厄：**我猜猜…他们宁愿忍耐，也不愿求变？

**Mydei:** ...That's right. The Mountain Dwellers were fearful of even the smallest change to their lifestyle, let alone leaving their home. Despite the imminent war, the elders would rather negotiate for peace instead of leaving the mountains. This made Geocles both angry and sad.

> **万敌：**…没错。山之民畏惧改变，更遑论迁徙。战争将至，长老却只打算求和，不愿离开山里。吉奥刻勒斯感到悲哀，愤怒。

**Mydei:** But he didn't give up. If you were in his shoes, what would you have done?

> **万敌：**但他并未放弃。若你是他，会怎么做？

**Phainon:** Lead my people on a resistance, perhaps? I'm not sure. I can't think of a solution that would make everyone happy.

> **白厄：**带领族人发起反抗？说不上来，一时想不到什么两全其美的办法。

**Mydei:** "A solution that would make everyone happy," huh... That's how naive we are. Well, let me tell you what Geocles did.

> **万敌：**「两全其美」——这正是你我的天真之处。而吉奥刻勒斯…我告诉你他是如何做的。

**Mydei:** He took the Mountain Dwellers' store of gemstones and traded them with the Ladonians for a massive amount of gunpowder — but he didn't do this to fight the Campanians. Under the cover of night, he set ablaze the forests surrounding the settlement, forcing his people to flee the mountain.

> **万敌：**他拿走了山之民囤积的宝石，找拉冬人交换了大量火药。但不是为了抗击坎帕人——趁着夜色，他点燃聚落周围的山林，逼迫所有人逃出了山野。

**Mydei:** That great fire marked the beginning of Mountain Dweller history. Since then, their people became part of civilization, and giants became a common sight in the holy city. Meanwhile, that mountain they once lived in has turned into little more than foam inside the black tide.

> **万敌：**那场大火成了山之民历史的起点，自那之后，他们才汇入文明的大河，圣城中才出现了巨人的身影。而曾经的那片山岳，早已成了黑潮中的几点浮沫。

**Phainon:** But leaving their homeland also brought many Mountain Dwellers misfortune. They —

> **白厄：**但离开故土，也让许多山之民遭遇不幸。比如……

**Mydei:** They were hunted by Kremnoans or enslaved. Indeed, that is undeniably also part of their history.

> **万敌：**…被悬锋族人猎捕，或沦为奴仆。没错，那亦是历史的一部分。

**Mydei:** But that is Geocles for you. The merits of his actions — or the lack thereof — have been scrutinized by many, but historians nevertheless hail him as the Mountainbreaker. Why do you think that is?

> **万敌：**这便是吉奥刻勒斯，他的功过自有人评说。但历史依然奉其为「开山者」，你觉得这是为何？

**Mydei:** It's simply because he took action and enacted change with his own hands. He didn't try to make everyone understand him, nor did he even waste time trying to make everyone happy.

> **万敌：**这道理再直白不过——因为他行动了，用自己的双手刻写下变革。他没有试图让所有人理解自己，也从未在两全其美的困境中蹉跎。

**Mydei:** He started a great fire and burned the shackles of tradition to ashes, and then... forced everyone to march toward the coming of a new era.

> **万敌：**他用一把大火烧毁了名为「传统」的枷锁，然后驱迫所有人…迈向滚滚而来的时代。

**Phainon:** ...You've made up your mind, haven't you?

> **白厄：**…你已下定决心了，是么？

**Mydei:** I'll be leaving Okhema soon to fight the greatest darkness of this world... and to shoulder Nikador's destiny.

> **万敌：**不久之后，我就会离开奥赫玛，去和这世间的至暗搏斗，去背负…尼卡多利的命运。

**Mydei:** So, listen well: If there comes a day when we meet again on the battlefield, and I stand opposed to the flame-chase...

> **万敌：**所以，仔细听好了——如果有一天，我们又在战场上相见，而我站在了逐火的对立面……

**Mydei:** Remember to stab your sword into my back through my tenth thoracic vertebra. That's my weak spot, and the only way to kill me.

> **万敌：**记得从背后刺入我的第十节胸椎，那是唯一能杀死我的弱点。

**Phainon:** ...

> **白厄：**……

**Phainon:** You have my word.

> **白厄：**一言为定。

**Mydei:** Go now. Your battlefield calls. Send that flame-thieving hyena to their death.

> **万敌：**走吧，奔向你的战场，为那盗火的鬣狗送去死亡。

**Mydei:** But if you find yourselves with no other recourse, then pray to that blade in the sky...

> **万敌：**但，倘若是你们被逼上了绝路，那就向天上的锋刃祈祷——

### Head to the battlefield

> **白厄：**为什么…阿格莱雅没告诉你们吗？我们受命前来协助各位，围猎盗火行者。

> **力竭的悬锋人：**阿格莱雅…天杀的僭主，我呸！

**Exhausted Kremnoan:** Our king... will be back soon... He'll be able to... deal with you... and that black-robed...

> **力竭的悬锋人：**我们的王…就要回来了。如果是他，一定能把你们…那个黑衣的……

A hoarse curse lodged in his throat, refusing to escape. Moments later, he convulsed, his head drooping as his spirit departed his body, drifting towards the abode of Thanatos.

**Phainon:** ...The Kremnoans really are just like their king.

> **白厄：**悬锋人…果真和他们的王如出一辙。

**Phainon:** Rest in peace. May your glory last forever.

> **白厄：**安息吧，愿你的荣光长存。

### Report back to Aglaea

> *Depleting her divine power, Trianne manages to send Tribbie and Trinnon away from the battle. The heroes gather in the city to plan tactics for rescuing Trianne and hunting down the Flame Reaver.*

*(Approach Aglaea)*

> **阿格莱雅：**不如说，这才是历史真实的模样：沉眠千年，只待觉醒的一瞬。可惜，现在不是研讨史观的时候。

**Aglaea:** I do not see Anaxa around. Was this under your instructions, Phainon?

> **阿格莱雅：**那刻夏不在，是你授意的吗，白厄？

> **白厄：**…是。相比旁观你们两个吵得不可开交，我认为这样更有效率。

**Aglaea:** A rash decision. After all, he still bears a Coreflame with him.

> **阿格莱雅：**这很鲁莽，他身上毕竟还携带着一颗火种。

**Aglaea:** I do concur with your assessment of efficiency, though. Now, let us table the issue concerning him for the moment. The more urgent matter is the Coreflame of Oronyx.

> **阿格莱雅：**但关于提升效率的判断…恐怕你是对的。他的问题先放到一边，当务之急是欧洛尼斯的火种，此事尤为棘手。

**Aglaea:** The Chrysos Heirs had initially crafted a plan regarding the order to return the Coreflames. For Titans like Oronyx and Cerces, who harbor no intentions of harming humanity, our strategy entailed assuming their divinity only at the very end.

> **阿格莱雅：**涉及火种归还的次序，黄金裔原本制定了战略。对于那些不会主动为害人间的泰坦——譬如欧洛尼斯和瑟希斯——我们本计划最后接过它们的神职。

**Aglaea:** But this unforeseen turn of events has thrown everything into disarray.

> **阿格莱雅：**但这场异变打乱了一切。

**Tribbie:** If we don't return the Coreflames to the Vortex of Genesis and we leave the Titans' divine positions vacant... we can't even begin to imagine the disaster that would follow!

> **缇宝：**如果不把火种归还涡心，让泰坦的神位就这么空着…不知会有怎样的劫难发生！

**Tribbie:** But the most pressing issue at hand... is that we haven't found the Chrysos Heir capable of bearing Oronyx's divinity.

> **缇宝：**可如今最大的问题…是我们还没找到能背负欧洛尼斯神职的黄金裔。

**Aglaea:** Phainon, Castorice, Mydei, and Hyacine... Tribbie and I devoted a millennium scouring the world, only to find these few people.

> **阿格莱雅：**白厄、遐蝶、万敌、风堇…我和吾师在世间寻觅千年，才终于找到了他们。

> **阿格莱雅：**万中选一的适格者，黄金裔中的魁首，若不能确信这些人拥有背负神权的潜质，我和吾师断然不会发出邀请。

**Aglaea:** Yet we cannot compel anyone into divinity, as it would be no different from sentencing them to the gallows.

## 112　Throne, End Those Long Years Forlorn　王座啊，终了那数载索寞

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域："Eternal Holy City" Okhema　|　角色：Mydei; Krateros; Aglaea; Hyacine; Castorice; Chartonus; Trailblazer; Dan Heng; Andriskos; Marsyas; Demetri; Phainon; Gorgo; Perdikkas; Hephaestion; Ptolemy; Leonnius; Peucesta　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Nemesis, Scorched by Golden Blood'' completed

### Speak with Krateros

> *The hunt for the Flame Reaver ends in victory for the Chrysos Heirs. Now bearing the mission of attaining divinity, you face another crucial choice: Claim the throne or return home.*

**Krateros:** ...

**Mydei:** I see the Reaper has forsaken you, too, teacher.

> **万敌：**出发前，还有些人要见。

**Krateros:** Hmph, it simply put my life in the hands of the Twilight Courtyard.

**Krateros:** That priest girl's medical talents are truly exceptional... She might be far from the ordinary girl we envisioned.

**Krateros:** But this isn't about me, Mydeimos. I saw it... You've finally decided to embrace your own fate.

**Mydei:** The power of Strife is stirring a tempest within me as we speak... My bones have turned into steel, and my blood is seething and ablaze. Past rulers of Kremnos could only aspire to this might, yet I have merged with it.

**Mydei:** I've made history, and henceforth, divinity and kingship shall coexist as one.

**Krateros:** Indeed. You've realized the aspirations of every former king of Kremnos.

**Krateros:** And now, you can at last bring our people home and reforge the glory of Kremnos.

**Mydei:** ...Unfortunately, I harbor no such desire.

> **万敌：**这不正是你想要的么？

**Krateros:** Wha—What do you mean by that?

**Mydei:** Over two millennia past, devotees of Nikador's might banded together beneath their feet and erected a city of their own. Ever since then, Kremnoans have served as the blades wielded by the god of war, following the Titan's path in its conquest.

> **万敌：**在圣城的时间，我一直在向你学习：制定战略，领导众人，回应变数…但直到最近，我才看清了你真正令人诚服的特质。

**Mydei:** Yet, gazing upon our history through the lens of my newfound divinity, I now see it with unprecedented clarity.

> **万敌：**你身上的「引力」。人们会自愿聚集在你的身边，聆听你的指引。

**Mydei:** This history is sheer absurdity and inferiority. People surged onto the battlefield like ants, plundering and slaying for their own greed, only to be trampled upon... like ants.

> **万敌：**他们不想令你失望，并非出于对惩戒的恐惧，而是发自内心地认同你的理念。

**Mydei:** The city and the beliefs that Kremnoans hold so dearly, alongside the supposed tradition... are mere anthills that can be crushed with a single touch in the eyes of the Titan.

**Krateros:** You...!

**Krateros:** Are you trying to strip Kremnoans of the pride we've amassed over thousands of years with mere words?!

> **阿格莱雅：**你已经成了这世上最强大的半神，迈德漠斯。你打算如何使用这份力量？

**Mydei:** ...That pride holds little significance, Krateros. Now that I've assumed the divinity of Strife, my next step...

> **万敌：**我将完成尼卡多利未竟的事业，成为翁法罗斯最坚固的墙垣，抵御黑潮的袭击。

**Mydei:** Will be to renounce the title of "King."

> **万敌：**我会为逐火之旅争取足够的时间，直到你带领所有人走到「创世」的奇迹面前。

**Krateros:** ...No! NO!

**Mydei:** Convey my message to every Kremnoan — this is an order.

**Krateros:** Do not do this, Mydeimos...

**Krateros:** I beg you...!

**Mydei:** Our history commenced in Year 2506 of the Light Calendar and concluded in Year 4931. I, Mydeimos, last King of Castrum Kremnos, son of Gorgo, do hereby proclaim...

**Mydei:** The Kremnoan dynasty comes to an official close today.

> **万敌：**…呵，果然是不知所谓的末日预告。

**Krateros:** You have killed us...

**Krateros:** You have killed us all...

**Mydei:** No, I am granting you a new beginning.

**Krateros:** What is this...

> **阿格莱雅：**为何发笑？

**Mydei:** My mother's signet ring. It served as a symbol of kingship, and I now imbue it with a fresh significance.

> **万敌：**我只是在想，换做常人看见这行字句，恐怕此生都不会再接近浴场。

**Mydei:** Take it and rally those who have lost their identities. Convey the words of Mydeimos, now ascended to divinity:

**Mydei:** You no longer need to chase fleeting honors or face death on the battlefield as if it were your sole destination. However, you must adapt to fit into this city that you once observed with indifferent eyes.

**Mydei:** A cruel fate awaits Amphoreus. No matter how illustrious a dynasty may seem, it crumbles to rubble and gravel before formidable foes.

> **万敌：**但如果我会被一则凭空出现的谵语吓破了胆，当初就不会受到你的注视，加入逐火的旅路。

**Mydei:** Yet, you shall tread the only path out, one that leads to a brand-new world at its end.

**Krateros:** Why task me with being your messenger? Why not explain all this to them yourself?

**Mydei:** Because only humans can guide the path to be trodden by humans. It's not the lot of "humans" who crave the power of gods and spiral into madness for it...

**Mydei:** But "humans" who stand ready to raise their shields high to protect the lives behind them.

**Krateros:** ...

**Krateros:** I failed to save that child.

**Mydei:** That is irrelevant. As demigods, they have long been prepared for it.

**Mydei:** What truly matters is that you showed up there and shielded her.

**Krateros:** The one who persuaded Aglaea to set me free that night... Was that you, Mydeimos?

**Mydei:** Yes.

**Krateros:** She's not one to easily make a concession. What did you offer her?

**Mydei:** I staked my mother's name to guarantee the integrity of your soul to her, and...

**Mydei:** ...I promised her that I would dedicate all I have to the Flame-Chase Journey.

**Krateros:** And what is next, Mydeimos...?

**Krateros:** ...Where will you be headed now?

**Mydei:** To the place where I belong, to fulfill my promise.

**Mydei:** The madness that has plagued Nikador and the darkness that has consumed Amphoreus...

**Mydei:** It's now my turn to fight against it.

### Bid farewell to Aglaea

> *You decisively reject the Kremnoans' centuries-old obsession with glory, opting to return home alone and assume Nikador's duty against the black tide. Before starting your homeward journey, you need to say goodbye to your friends in the city.*

**Mydei:** There are some people to meet with before I leave.

> **万敌：**也对…你一直是我们当中最乐观的那个。

**Mydei:** They strive not to disappoint you, not due to fear of repercussions, but out of sincere alignment with your ideals.

> **万敌：**如此说来，倒是有一句话想求你转述。

> **万敌：**——告诉她们，以后不必再以肉身勘察敌后了。只需一条讯息，天谴的锋刃便会指向奥赫玛的敌人。

**Aglaea:** ...I am truly honored to hear that.

**Aglaea:** And now, Mydeimos, you have risen as the most powerful demigod in the world. How do you intend to wield this newfound power?

**Mydei:** I shall see Nikador's aborted mission through to completion, and become the strongest bulwark for Amphoreus to defend against the black tide.

> **万敌：**我先你一步走进自己的宿命了，遐蝶。

**Mydei:** I will secure enough time for the Flame-Chase Journey until you manage to lead everyone to reach the miracle of "Genesis."

**Aglaea:** Mydeimos, a new chapter awaits Amphoreus... Yet, the one who guides the people to the destination need not be you and I.

**Mydei:** Hehe...

**Mydei:** What did you see after assuming Mnestia's divinity?

> **万敌：**死亡拒绝引渡我。若你将我送往冥界，或许我能在被遣返的路上找到些线索。

**Aglaea:** ...Despite the passage of a thousand years, every word of that prophecy remains as vivid as ever.

**Aglaea:** "You shall have your final bath in warm and radiant gold."

**Mydei:** ...Heh, just as cryptic as all the other prophecies.

> **万敌：**…你说得对。我的族人之所以挣扎千年，或许正是因为他们太过轻视死亡。

**Mydei:** Haha...

**Aglaea:** What's the reason for the laughter?

**Mydei:** I was just thinking... if an ordinary person encountered these words, they would probably stay away from the baths for the rest of their life.

> **万敌：**再努力些吧，遐蝶。去完成你必须完成的事，让你我这一路承受的磨难有所收获。

**Aglaea:** But I cannot forgo the delightful experience a bath offers.

**Aglaea:** So, I assume you also glimpsed yours, Mydeimos?

**Mydei:** ...

**Mydei:** "One day, you shall die with a wound in your back."

> **万敌：**我将担起你们的信任，令世界不再受恐惧主宰。

**Aglaea:** Well, that is a rather straightforward scenario.

**Mydei:** If I were to be terrified by drivel with no basis, I would not have attracted your gaze and joined the Flame-Chase Journey in the first place.

> **万敌：**但哈托努斯，请你继续关照白厄。我有一种预感，也许他的命运…比我们所有人加起来都更沉重。

**Aglaea:** You speak the truth. May you be destined for a powerful fate, and may we reunite in the promised new world...

> **哈托努斯：**白厄的事，我会记得。归乡去吧，勇敢的王。

**Aglaea:** Farewell, Mydei.

> **万敌：**我无需读心的能力，也能戳穿你的把戏。

> **白厄：**也许，我根本没有斩断自己的宿命。

> **万敌：**但那不重要。痛苦可以治愈，伤痕却不能，也不应被磨灭。即便借助欧洛尼斯的神力，过去也会在视线的死角留下蛛丝马迹。

> **万敌：**就算斩杀了那黑衣人，你心中的失落便能一笔勾销？不可能。复仇只是一件必须被完成的任务，但没有人能通过它得到心灵的满足。

**Phainon:** ...You're right.

> **白厄：**…谁说不是呢。

**Mydei:** Look forward. Chew up and swallow your past, then make sure emptiness and bitterness are not the only flavors you know.

> **万敌：**向前看。把你的过往嚼碎、咽下，勿让空虚与苦涩成为你唯一熟知的滋味。

> **万敌：**别忘了，你身后还有一整个世界等着被拯救。

> **万敌：**最后一个要求，代我照顾好悬锋的战士们。

> **万敌：**融入奥赫玛的过程中，他们一定会遇到阻力…我唯一的遗憾，便是无法与他们同担这段时光。

**Phainon:** Do not worry. Leave it to me.

> **白厄：**放心，交给我吧。

**Phainon:** And while I'm at it, I'm going to find out if the Kremnoan language is really missing that many words.

> **白厄：**我倒要找他们确认下，看看悬锋人的字典里是不是真的缺了那么多词语。

**Mydei:** Hmph, the Kremnoan philosophy can never be encapsulated in a mere dictionary.

> **万敌：**哼，悬锋人的哲学从来不以字典彰显。

**Mydei:** But... if there's a chance in the next life, you should come visit my library.

> **万敌：**但…来世若有机会，来我的图书馆多看看吧。

**Mydei:** I'll be on my way, Deliverer. Remember to stay alive till the final act.

> **万敌：**走了，救世主——记得活到最后。

**Phainon:** Yes.

> **白厄：**嗯。

**Phainon:** Same goes for you. Don't die too easily —

> **白厄：**你也是，别死了——

**Phainon:** May triumph always be yours, Mydeimos.

> **白厄：**祝你战无不胜，迈德漠斯。

**Mydei:** ...Oh, right.

> **万敌：**…对了。

**Mydei:** One last question.

> **万敌：**最后一个问题。

**Phainon:** Go ahead. You better not leave any regrets behind.

> **白厄：**问吧，别留遗憾。

**Mydei:** Are you the one who told Chartonus about the signet ring?

> **万敌：**告诉哈托努斯那枚印戒存在的人，就是你吧？

**Phainon:** Well...

> **白厄：**这个嘛……

**Phainon:** Who knows?

> **白厄：**谁知道呢？

*(Enter Exploratory Excursion Homebound Path)*

...

**Mydei:** Hah... Hah...

> **万敌：**哈…哈……

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: Get up, my son! You showed great courage in today's training. Let's wrap it up for now.

**Mydei:** Yes, Mother.

> **万敌：**是，母亲。

**Mydei:** Mother... There's something I want to ask you.

> **万敌：**母亲…我有一件事想问。

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: What is it, Mydeimos?

**Mydei:** Why must we learn to fight?

> **万敌：**为什么我们生来就要学习战斗？

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: It is for glory and honor, my son. For Kremnoans, mastery of swords and spears is ingrained from birth, and the battlefield is our destined end.

**Mydei:** Is that really the way it is, Mother?

> **万敌：**真是如此吗，母亲？

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: What makes you say so?

**Mydei:** Because you don't sound entirely sure.

> **万敌：**因为你听上去不太肯定。

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: ...You're right, Mydeimos.

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: I once believed those words without question until your father cast you into the Sea of Souls.

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: It was at that moment I realized how everything I believed in was utterly hollow. Perhaps the spirit of Kremnos had once existed... but as greed bloomed in the hearts of humanity over the years, such spirit had long faded alongside our glory.

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: I no longer put my faith in any oath or doctrine. Now, I have just one role...

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: That of your mother, Mydeimos. Your guardian...

**Mydei:** ...That dream always ends there. The journey back to Castrum Kremnos is proving to be longer than expected.

> **万敌：**…梦总会结束于此。返回悬锋城的路途也比我预想更久。

**Mydei:** Krateros said that, shortly after my fall into the sea, my mother challenged my father to a duel — She died through Father's treachery, and I never saw her face.

> **万敌：**克拉特鲁斯说，在我坠海后不久，母亲便向先王发起了角斗——她死于父亲的毒计，我与她素未谋面。

**Mydei:** Yet each night, she would show up, waiting by the flickering firelight. I always anticipated the sequel to that dream, believing she had more words of wisdom or guidance to offer...

> **万敌：**但她会在每个夜晚出现，等候在朦胧的火光中。我始终在等待梦的后续，以为她一定还会留下更多嘱托，或者指引……

**Mydei:** But now... I think I have finally come to understand. That is all she wished to tell me.

> **万敌：**但现在…我似乎明白了。这便是她想对我表达的全部。

**Mydei:** And now, I too am left with but one role…

> **万敌：**如今，我只剩下一个身份……

### Return home

> **帕狄卡斯：**看啊，迈德漠斯回来了！

> **赫菲斯辛：**新鲜的石榴汁，一起尝尝嘛？

> **伙伴们：**欢迎回来！

**Mydei:** ...

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: Welcome home... Mydeimos.

Gorgo, Mydei's Mother: Have you found something deserving of your protection as well?

**Crowd:** The son of Gorgo will be crowned in blood!

**Crowd:** The son of Gorgo will be crowned in blood!

> **万敌：**母亲啊……

Year 4931 of the Light Calendar, Month of Balance Nikador — the Strife Titan, Lance of Fury — fell. Mydeimos, king of Kremnos, triumphed over the trial, and the new god was born.

On the following day, the Kremnoan dynasty, which had lasted for a thousand years, came to an end.

Returning to the Trailblazer's POV...

When you have a chance to make a choice, make one that you know you won't regret.

## 113　Passage, Reveal the Past Once More　来路啊，请再度显映往履

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域："Eternal Holy City" Okhema　|　角色：Trailblazer; Tribbie; Trinnon; Castorice; Phainon; Aglaea; Tribios; Oronyx; Mortis; Janus; Anaxa; Cerces; Dan Heng; March 7th　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Throne, End Those Long Years Forlorn'' completed

### Participate in Trianne's funeral

> *Though we won the fight against the Flame Reaver, we have lost Trianne forever.*

**Tribbie:** We smell sunlight in the wind of Okhema...

> **缇宝：**奥赫玛的风中有阳光的味道……

**Tribbie:** This was your favorite spot, wasn't it, Trianne?

> **缇宝：**你以前最喜欢这里了。对吧，缇安？

**Castorice:** Lady Trianne... Why did it have to end up like this?

> **遐蝶：**缇安大人，为什么…会变成这样呢？

**Tribbie:** Take it easy, Cas. Departure is a part of life. It's just that ours isn't as tumultuous as it is for ordinary folks.

> **缇宝：**轻松些，蝶。人生总有离去，只不过*我们*的告别，来得不如常人那般暴烈。

**Trinnon:** When we started our journey, Janus had already provided their prophecy. Just like how that Titan divided themself to create the countless passages in the world...

> **缇宁：**在*我们*踏上旅途时，雅努斯就已降下预言。就像它分裂自身，化作世间万千门径……

**Trinnon:** It's only natural for us to follow the same path since we inherited their destiny.

> **缇宁：** *我们*接过它的命运，走上相同的轨迹也是理所当然。

**Phainon:** If you don't mind, Tribbie...

> **白厄：**缇宝老师，如果不介意……

**Phainon:** Are you still willing to unveil that past for us? The past regarding the "prophecy."

> **白厄：**你还愿意为我们揭露那段历史么？有关「神谕」的过往。

**Tribbie:** What do you seek to learn, Snowy?

> **缇宝：**小白想了解的是什么呢？

**Phainon:** ...

> **白厄：**……

**Phainon:** Mydeimos has left the city to face his own fate.

> **白厄：**迈德漠斯出发了，去背负属于他的命运。

**Phainon:** That is the fate of every demigod. We will all eventually let go of our past to follow the same path as his.

> **白厄：**这是每一位半神的使命。终有一日，我们都要卸下过往，踏上和他同样的道路。

**Phainon:** So, what I want to know is — As Amphoreus' first demigod, what compelled you to take the first step on this journey?

> **白厄：**所以，我想知道——身为翁法罗斯第一位半神，是怎样的勇气让你迈出了这条路上最初的一步。

**Phainon:** And what has sustained you to make it this far without looking back, despite the fracturing of your body and the numerous departures you have endured?

> **白厄：**又是什么支撑着你，在打碎自身，经历无数离别后，依旧能义无反顾地走到今天。

**Tribbie:** ...Alright.

> **缇宝：**…可以呀。

**Aglaea:** Teacher, are you sure about this?

> **阿格莱雅：**吾师，没关系么？

**Tribbie:** De is already doing what he has to do... What reason do we have to keep it from them? Let's go, everyone...

> **缇宝：**小敌都已经身体力行了…我们还有什么理由瞒着大家呢？走吧，各位——

- ▸ It looks like a specialty from your hometown.

> **缇宁：** *我们*第一次见它，还是很小的时候。妈妈带了一块好看的玻璃回来，可她脸上挂着泪痕，随手就把它放在桌上。

**Trinnon:** It looked so sparkly that we couldn't resist playing with it, but we ended up breaking it by accident. Mother didn't get angry. She simply held us quietly for a long time.

> **缇宁：** *我们*觉得它亮晶晶的，就拿来玩，结果不小心打碎了…妈妈也没说什么，只是抱着*我们*沉默了很久。

> **缇宁：**长大后*我们*才知道，在雅努萨波利斯，如果有人为命运光荣赴死，圣女就要从火山的采石场中撷下一块玻璃，细细打磨，交由死者家属接收。

**Trinnon:** On the day Mother returned with the glass, her friend lost his life on the battlefield. Since he had no family left, Mother had to keep it herself...

> **缇宁：**妈妈带着它回来的那天，她的朋友倒在了战场上。他没有活着的家人，这块玻璃就只能由她自己收留……

**Trinnon:** Later, on the eve of the ritual... Mother also gave us a beautiful piece of glass.

> **缇宁：**后来，妈妈在仪式前夜…也给了*我们*一块美丽的玻璃。

> **温柔的母亲：**没错，我的好女孩。怎么了？

**Tribios:** I wish I could go too... I have something to ask Janus.

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**呜，我也想去…我有问题想问雅努斯。

**Gentle Mother:** Oh? What do you want to ask?

> **温柔的母亲：**哦？是什么问题呢？

**Tribios:** I want to know how to chase the black tide away!

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**我想问，怎么才能赶走黑潮！

**Gentle Mother:** Ah, I see. Unfortunately, it's a secret ritual, and only I can preside over it.

> **温柔的母亲：**这样啊。不过很可惜，这是一场秘密仪式，只有妈妈才能主持。

**Gentle Mother:** If you want me to consult the Titan on your behalf... you'll have to promise me something.

> **温柔的母亲：**如果你想让妈妈帮你问问泰坦的想法…那就必须答应一件事。

**Tribios:** What is it?

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**是什么事？

**Gentle Mother:** Be a good girl and go to sleep now, and whatever you dream of, don't be afraid—

> **温柔的母亲：**你要乖乖睡觉，无论梦到什么，都不要害怕——

**Gentle Mother:** Just sleep through the night, until morning comes, until the next time opens their eyes. Can you do that for me, sweetie?

> **温柔的母亲：**就这样，一觉睡到大清早，睡到明天，艾格勒下一次睁眼的时候。能做到吗，亲爱的？

**Tribios:** That's it? Of course I can! Tribios is the most amazing Holy Maiden of Janus... Well, little Holy Maiden!

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**这么简单，当然可以啦。缇里西庇俄丝可是最厉害的雅努斯圣女…小圣女！

**Tribios:** But... why, Mama?

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**可是，为什么呢？

**Gentle Mother:** Because... this ritual might take a long time.

> **温柔的母亲：**因为呀…这场仪式，可能会持续很久。

**Tribios:** How long?

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**有多久？

**Gentle Mother:** Tomorrow, my sweet child. I'll be back tomorrow.

> **温柔的母亲：**我的好孩子…明天，妈妈就会回来。

**Gentle Mother:** If not tomorrow, then the next tomorrow. If not the tomorrow after that. But if I still haven't returned for a very long time...

> **温柔的母亲：**但如果明天来不及，那就明天的明天；如果明天的明天也不行，就等到下一个明天。如果过了很久很久，妈妈都没回来……

**Gentle Mother:** That means the ritual has succeeded. It means I've found a way to chase away the black tide and reached the new world on the other side. But for various reasons, I can't return to bring my sweet girl with me.

> **温柔的母亲：**那就说明仪式已经成功，妈妈找到了赶走黑潮的办法，去到了彼岸那片崭新的天地。只是因为许多原因，妈妈没办法回过头，接上我的好女孩一起。

**Gentle Mother:** But I'll be waiting for you there. As long as you stay brave and kind, we'll meet again in that new world.

> **温柔的母亲：**但妈妈会在那边等你，只要你做个勇敢、善良的孩子，我们就一定能在那片新天地重逢。

**Gentle Mother:** It's the end of the west wind, the black tide's other shore, a radiant sea of flowers blessed and protected by the gods.

> **温柔的母亲：**那里呀，是西风的尽头，黑潮的彼岸，受众神赐福护佑的、熠熠生辉的花海。

**Gentle Mother:** In the rosy horizon, you'll see a silver-white shoal. It marks the end of your voyage — a haven free from storms, cold, and heavy rains. No sorrow can linger there... That is where I'll be waiting for you.

> **温柔的母亲：**在玫瑰色的天际中，你会看到一片银白的浅滩。那是旅途的终点，没有风雪、严寒、骤雨，没有人会受到悲伤的感染…妈妈就在那里等你。

**Tribios:** Wow! That sounds just like the island from my dream!

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**哇，听起来就像梦里的小岛！

**Tribios:** Oh, I know! I'll turn the moon into a ship and the stars into sails to go find Mama! Pinky promise?

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**我明白了——我会乘着月亮，扬起星星做的船帆去找妈妈！那我们约好了哦？

**Gentle Mother:** Mmm, pinky promise.

> **温柔的母亲：**嗯，约好了。

**Gentle Mother:** As long as you sail toward tomorrow, as long as the sun will rise for the next day...

> **温柔的母亲：**只要向着明天出发，只要明天的太阳还会升起……

**Gentle Mother:** Our wishes will come true together.

> **温柔的母亲：**我们的愿望，就会一同实现。

**Tribios:** If the final murmurs of the Titan are true...

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**如果泰坦陨落时的呢喃并非戏言……

**Tribios:** If such a place really exists on the shore beyond the black tide and at the end of "Genesis"...

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**如果黑潮的彼岸，「创世」的尽头，真的存在那样一片天地……

**Tribios:** Then our promise still stands.

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**那我们的约定就依然还在。

**Tribios:** ...Please wait for me, Mother.

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**…等我，母亲。

**Trinnon:** After those ten years of darkness, when Aquila opened their eyes for the last time...

> **缇宁：**在那暗无天日的十年间，艾格勒最后一次睁眼时……

**Trinnon:** We finally set off.

> **缇宁：** *我们*出发了。

**Small Boy:** Shh, shh! Please keep it down! We had to be really sneaky to get in here!

> **小男孩：**嘘，嘘！大姐姐，声音轻点！我们好不容易才偷偷溜进来。

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**好，我知道啦。不过，你们专程溜进来，是想……

**Little Girl:** I heard there's a lovely Holy Maiden living in the temple, and I wanna meet her!

> **小女孩：**我听说神殿里面住着一位漂亮的圣女大人，想要见见她！

**Small Boy:** I couldn't stop her, so I had to think of a way to sneak her in.

> **小男孩：**我拗不过她，只好想办法带她溜进来了。

**Tribios:** Oh, really? I was here to meet the Holy Maiden as well! But the guard said she had to go somewhere far away today.

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**这样啊！我也是来见圣女大人的。可惜听卫兵说，她今天要出远门呢。

**Little Girl:** Huh? What a bummer! Mommy even asked me to bring her gift to the Holy Maiden...

> **小女孩：**啊，怎么这样…妈妈还托我带了礼物呢……

**Tribios:** It's getting late! Be good and go home now, okay?

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**乖，时候不早啦，快回家去吧。

**Little Girl:** Okay, then. We'll come back next time! Thank you!

> **小女孩：**好吧，那我们下次再来！谢谢你，大姐姐。

**Trinnon:** By the way they are dressed, the siblings look like civilians living outside the temple. Mother mentioned that when she was a Holy Maiden, she caught a girl sneaking into the temple to steal the tributes.

> **缇宁：**这两个孩子…看打扮是在圣殿外生活的平民。听妈妈说，她还是圣女的时候，曾经有个女孩闯进神殿，想要偷贡品，被她看见了。

**Trinnon:** Stealing from the temple was punishable by death, but that poor girl had no family and just wanted something to eat. So, Mother took her outside and even gave her some food.

> **缇宁：**在神殿偷窃是死罪，可那女孩年纪还小，无依无靠，只是想填饱肚子。妈妈就把她抱出了神殿，还悄悄塞给她一些好吃的。

**Trinnon:** Perhaps these two children are the descendants of that girl from back then.

> **缇宁：**也许，眼前这两个孩子…就是当年那女孩的后代呢。

*(Approach the Amiable Woman)*

**Amiable Woman:** Ah, Lady Maiden!

> **慈祥的妇人：**啊，圣女大人！

*(Interact with Tribios)*

**Amiable Woman:** Lady Maiden, you need to dress warmer! It's pretty cold out. Let me get you a blanket...

> **慈祥的妇人：**圣女大人，你怎么穿得这么薄就出来了？今天天冷，我去取条毯子来……

**Tribios:** No need to trouble yourself. I'm just going for a quick walk.

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**不用啦，女士，我就是出来散散步，马上就回去。

**Amiable Woman:** ...Alright, but please take care of yourself. Your mother made us promise to look after you before she left.

> **慈祥的妇人：**唉，好吧…你可要保重身体。令堂临行前对我们千叮咛万嘱咐，一定要照顾好你呢。

**Amiable Woman:** Hehe, it's been a while since I last saw you, and I just realized you've grown into such a beauty, just like your mother.

> **慈祥的妇人：**呵呵，说到这个才发觉…许久没见，你竟已出落得和你母亲一般美丽了啊。

**Amiable Woman:** Here, have some of my sweet pastries. She used to enjoy them, so maybe you'll like them too.

> **慈祥的妇人：**来来，吃些甜饼吧！她以前很喜欢我做的点心，或许你和她口味相仿呢。

**Tribios:** I...

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**这……

**Amiable Woman:** What's wrong, Lady Maiden?

> **慈祥的妇人：**圣女大人，怎么了？

**Tribios:** I'm... fine. It tastes... really good.

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**我…没事，女士。这甜饼…真好吃。

**Trinnon:** This lady is Mother's old friend. When we were little, we sometimes got to eat the sweet pastries she brought back.

**Trinnon:** We've always remembered the familiar taste, and only now do we learn where it came from.
